An id from plugin code that is not one (`for`, a decision's or a run's id, a task filter's `goal` or `worker`), and a task filter's `state` or `dueBy` that is not one, are refused before the database is asked, as nothing found or as `plugin.invalid_args`, where they failed the call. `createTestContext` takes `console.decisions.answer`, which it dropped.

- [`830adb9`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/830adb9f2bf53eb9054dd2b0baf952bc2d104af8) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A decision on My decisions is answered at its own address (`POST /api/v1/decisions/<id>/answer`, ADR-0068), as it is read there: one that reaches the person from a project they are not in, a plugin's up the reporting line or one they were named in before they left the project, was refused when answered from that page.

- [`85905e9`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/85905e9f7d301c02c4a8a9034420f202bcb9206b) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A person, a project or a goal is chosen by searching wherever one is chosen (ADR-0037), as a task's parent and its goal were: a person's manager on the People page, whom to add to a project, a worker on a task, the person and the goal the board is narrowed to, a plugin's `person` and `project` settings and their lists, the board project a plugin is enabled with, and whom a role is granted to, one field over everyone, the projects, the people and the plugins. Each is the `Picker`, searching the listing the page already holds; the chosen stands in the field's place and a press clears it. Short and fixed lists, a state, a priority, a category, a role, stay lists. `Picker` takes `id`, for a label to point at, and `listSearch`, `peopleSearch`, `projectsSearch` and `goalsSearch` are exported, so that a custom console searches its own listings the same way.

- [`2435384`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/2435384b029cc6401e0efb30ff03bf28ff16be3c) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - Teams (ADR-0073): a team is a key, a name and its members, a person in any number of them, kept by an administrator on the People page's view of the teams (`/people/teams`) and with `console teams` and `console team new <key> "<name>"|rename|remove|add|drop`. A team is called by its key (ADR-0074): a project key's shape, given when the team is made and never changed, and one among the teams' keys and the projects' keys of the workspace, so that a project is refused a team's key (`project.key_taken`) as a team is refused a project's (`team.key_taken`). `GET /api/v1/teams` lists them with the members the asker may see; `POST /api/v1/teams`, `PATCH` and `DELETE /api/v1/teams/<key>` and `POST|DELETE /api/v1/teams/<key>/members` keep them, and the client's `teams`, `createTeam`, `renameTeam`, `removeTeam`, `addTeamMember` and `removeTeamMember` call them; the people listing and `ctx.console.people` name each person's teams by key (`teams`), and `ctx.console.teams.list()` and `get(key)` read the teams, with `people:read`. A role of a plugin is granted to a team by its key (`team` on `POST .../grants`, `--team <key>`, the settings page), held by whoever is in it as the membership changes, and a grant answers the team as `{ key, name }`. Five new event kinds, `team.created`, `team.renamed`, `team.removed`, `team.member_added` and `team.member_removed`, each with the team's `key`, reach a plugin's hooks with `people:read:all` as the people's do. The fact the People page called a person's team is their direct reports now.

- [`c5b6662`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/c5b6662951d62b1c1f1ee0bf4a430735c4f8b83d) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The address Google vouches for at a sign-in is the person's work email wherever else it was written ([ADR-0069](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/blob/main/docs/adr/0069-the-directory-keeps-a-work-email-read-by-an-administrator-the-person-and-a-plugin-allowed-to.md)): another person of the workspace who held it gives it up, their `email` becoming `null`, whether an administrator wrote it on them, as on a deployment that signs people in at GitHub and at Google, or they are the person of a Workspace account made again under another id; the sign-in goes through either way. Each address a sign-in changes, the other person's and the person's own, writes `person.email_changed` with the login and whether an address is set, never the address, as an administrator's change does, so that a plugin that matches people by address matches them again.

- [`87a82b1`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/87a82b19479ae3e066f13291f5b377618b0a9d85) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A plugin's server reads the console's public origin and the addresses of its own endpoints, to register a callback with a service elsewhere and to write a link to the console: `ctx.console.workspace().url` is `PUBLIC_URL`, or `null` where the deployment names none, and `ctx.endpoints.url(name)` the full address of an endpoint the plugin defines (`plugin.not_found` for one it does not, `plugin.unavailable` without a public address). `createTestContext` takes `publicUrl` and `endpoints` to answer them in a plugin's tests. Neither needs a capability: nothing in them is secret.

- [`64a232a`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/64a232a1b39e77110671af1a6ee1a9e3fc584650) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The dispatcher delivers to a few plugins at a time, a quarter as many as the process has database connections, at least one and four at most, where it took every plugin with hooks at once: with as many of them as the pool has connections, four in the `worker` role, each held one for its lock and waited for another, every pass waited out the pool's timeout and delivered nothing, and with `WORKER_IN_PROCESS` the API's requests waited with it. A plugin's lock is held for as long as its delivery may take, where a pass of more than thirty seconds, a slow hook after twenty seconds of others, lost it to the server's bound on an idle transaction, and the process ended with the session, as it did under a job of more than thirty seconds; a session the server ends under a transaction now fails that transaction alone. No job shares a plugin's lock, where a job named `dispatch` held up its plugin's hooks while it ran. The hourly sweep of what is past a collection's retention goes through the collection a hundred records at a time, in the order they were made, for a minute an hour at most, where one statement for a whole collection with its history could outlast the statement timeout every hour and never finish; and a collection that cannot be swept is a line in the log, where it stopped the sweep of every plugin after it.

- [`a851776`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/a851776c216d2df4c8a7a91ed9bfc6e24eb2b80c) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - Breaking: the console takes commands from 0.4.5 on (ADR-0038, ADR-0043). The event `person.email_changed`, which the work email of the directory writes, is a kind an older command's reader refuses, so a console from 0.4.5 answers a command before 0.4.5 with `426` and `client.outdated`, naming the version to install (`npm install -g @skillcdn/console@latest`); the command's own floor stays at 0.4.2, since it reads an older console as before. The REST snapshot is taken again at the floors. The teams' events (`team.created`, `team.renamed`, `team.removed`, `team.member_added`, `team.member_removed`, ADR-0073) are among the kinds an older command's reader refuses.

- [`08e1c2e`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/08e1c2e6eeda8ec006a8a5d3bab3a8f0f7d3b810) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - `console plugin <key>` lists each command and query in brief: the positionals and the required flags, the rest as `[options]`, which `console plugin <key> help <command>` prints whole; `help` without a command lists them the same way. An agent reads the listing whole at every first use, and a plugin with many flags filled half of it with usage. A `file` argument prints as `--file <path>`, the flag the command takes, where it printed a flag of the argument's name that the command then refused. `formatOperationUsage(key, operation, { brief })` takes the option.

- [`2077146`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/2077146b449ceb0b74979a2ef4fcfa4a3e9e4e9f) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The People page reads at a glance, and the teams have a view of their own. A person's card lists their facts in one column of names and one of values: their role, manager and work email, which an administrator changes in place in quiet fields that read as values until the pointer or the focus is on them; whom they manage, as faces with how many; and their teams, as chips with the way to add one at the end of the row. The teams are a view of the People page beside the people's, `/people/teams`, with the form that makes one at `/people/teams/new`: a card per team with how many are in it and who, its name written anew in its place, a person added from the team's own card and taken out from their chip, and the way to delete a team, asked first, since the roles granted to it go with it. `TeamList` and `TeamForm` are exported, and `TeamList` is among the components a console may replace; `PeopleList` keeps the teams of a person (`teams`, `onJoinTeam`, `onLeaveTeam`) and no longer the teams themselves.
  
  `Chip` is a new block: a thing named among others in a row, a person, a team, an item of a list, a holder of a role, with the way to take it out where it can be. The workers of a task, the people a decision is for, a list setting of a plugin and the holders of a plugin's role are rows of it, chips are low pills of one shape wherever they are (`.sc-chip`, the holders of a role `sc-chip sc-plugin-holder`), and the way to add one more stands at the end of the row as a chip, a `Picker` with the new `className` `sc-picker-add`; `sc-picker-quiet` is a picker that reads as what it holds, as `.sc-input-quiet` is now for any field, a select or an input. A choice of a list setting is a pill lit in the accent with a tick. The words read better: a secret the console received says from which host, and until when; the time zone nobody set, the role that follows the reporting line and the board's filter of people read plainly in Korean.

- [`08e1c2e`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/08e1c2e6eeda8ec006a8a5d3bab3a8f0f7d3b810) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The preview console of `pnpm dev:plugins` answers as the console's own version, so that the real `console` command signs in to it (`console login --url <its address>`) and runs a plugin's commands as an agent would; it answered as the test harness's fixed version and refused the command as too new. It also serves the plugins' bundles uncached: a plugin rebuilt under one version was run by a browser from the build it had kept, against a server that answered something else, which read like a bug of the plugin's.

- [`3ac7663`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/3ac7663610af4c5f4c74997a57cd072c6f066f27) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - What the review before 0.4.5 found of grants and teams: purging a plugin takes away the roles it held in other plugins too (ADR-0072), each said as `plugin.revoked` on that plugin's board, so that a package installed later under the key holds nothing it was not granted; removing a team says each role granted to it as `plugin.revoked`, where it went without a word; a team's name is one line, a control or an invisible character refused (`request.invalid`, and `team.invalid_name` from the console's own check) as every other name is; two administrators changing the teams at once no longer deadlock, every write taking the workspace's row first. A frame's `open` never opens a page of the console's own origin, which is `navigate`'s (ADR-0067), and the grant's refusal names the five whom a role may go to.

## 0.4.4

### Patch Changes

- [`83ef42f`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/83ef42f59fef23b58ff93f9c26dda887654c5d4c) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A decision may be addressed to several people ([ADR-0057](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/blob/main/docs/adr/0057-a-decision-may-be-addressed-to-several-people-and-the-first-answer-ends-it.md)): `POST .../decisions` takes `deciderIds` beside `deciderId`, `console ask` takes `--to` more than once, the form takes several people where it took one, and a plugin's `ctx.console.decisions.ask` takes `deciderIds`; up to `MAX_DECIDERS` (10), each once and each able to work in the project (`decision.too_many_deciders`, `decision.invalid_decider`). Each of them finds the decision among their own, in `console work` and in the count beside My decisions; any of them may answer, and the first answer ends it. A decision answers `deciders`, everyone it is addressed to in the order named (read as optional, for an older console), beside `decider`, the first of them; the pages show each and the command prints their logins.

- [`68f0ec5`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/68f0ec5b1a98dd625ef7c5b3fdf778d3d41ee79a) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A decision raised by the version before during a rollout, after the migration and before the old process is replaced, reaches the person it is addressed to among their decisions and in the count beside My decisions; it came with no list of those it is addressed to, and was found only among the project's.

- [`6383d5e`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/6383d5ed95994e9cddde2a23806e16f6b09f57ab) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A failure reads in the person's language. The pages showed the server's message, which is English and written for programs; they now read the code in the language pack (`errors`, every code the server can answer). `errorWords(error, fallback, pack)` takes the pack, `wordsForFailure(error, pack)` answers the words or nothing, and `useConsoleData` carries the `failure` itself beside `error`. A frame is told a console's refusal in the same words, and a plugin's own refusal in the plugin's. A language pack of your own gains `errors`.

- [`099fa9a`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/099fa9aa9943ef541b2e4bfaa12286f0e1ad1b9f) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A plugin's frame keeps to its policy. It could go to a file of its own bundle, where the frame's policy no longer held, and an SVG there ran scripts that reached anywhere and still spoke to the bridge; a bundle's files now carry a policy of their own and run nothing as a document, while a picture stays a picture. A frame holds no WebRTC either, which no policy governs: every script of a bundle is served with a line before it that takes WebRTC out of the frame and of any frame made inside it, so that a source map is a line out and an `integrity` attribute of a plugin's own no longer matches. A browser or a cache on the way keeps the scripts of a version it fetched before this, which are immutable, so that this reaches a plugin already installed with its next version.

- [`cba312e`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/cba312e38d0eaa1f5a9a7f6a2d61daac6101c163) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A plugin that is off leaves the front page: an administrator, who reads every plugin installed, no longer finds its widgets there, and a frame the console has nothing to show in (a plugin turned off since the page read it, a page it has not) says so in the console's words, through the bridge's new `unavailable`, in place of an answer meant for a program.

- [`3be49ba`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/3be49ba4e29641580c68d13bef5c6d538c34ad77) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - `PATCH /api/v1/me` and `PATCH /api/v1/workspace` read at most 256 KiB, as the rest of the API does, and answer `request.too_large` past it.

- [`9dae8f1`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/9dae8f1ba732bf65407117f3e203a6b950db93f1) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A plugin reads the workspace's week: `frame.context.workspace` says the time zone, the `weekStart` setting and `firstDay`, the day a week begins on with the setting applied, 1 for Monday, 7 for Sunday, and `ctx.console.workspace()` says `weekStart`, so that a calendar in a plugin starts on the day the console's own do.

- [`ddcdf4f`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/ddcdf4f6278efff4f0550806f7c72118b713599f) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A plugin's tabs read as levels of one thing. Its pages go on from the header's tabs on the same gutters (`sc-page-tabs`), `sc-subtabs` around `sc-tab`s is now the console's view switch for the views within a page, and a page's frame stands one step (`--sc-space-4`) outside the page's column, so that a page that pads itself by that step, as the template does, lines up with the tabs above it. A plugin's page that pads itself by more, or by nothing, shows the difference.

- [`e267fd0`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/e267fd0ff6e30a5626632bbfcb7c7d4ddaad0857) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A plugin's thread has no way to the network but `ctx`, as it was meant to: `process.getBuiltinModule` answered the built-ins the module hook refuses, and `node:_http_client`, `node:_tls_wrap` and their kin, the names Node keeps for compatibility, were not refused at all. Both now fail as `node:net` does, and `console-plugin check` says so of a bundle that uses them.

- [`8a12869`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/8a1286922dc8804de5080c26c4840794ab117109) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A button given the class `sc-tab` reads like the console's tabs, so that a plugin's own tabs in its frame do too.

- [`53d54c4`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/53d54c46ef97565179565e45173dcd2baee5cb65) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A tile on the front page never cuts a line of words. Its mark and the room around it follow the cell, and a row that still does not fit stands whole or not at all. What waits on a project reads as the count on a tab does, and the tray of what was taken off says Add.

- [`ff335fd`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/ff335fd003d4edd3ab208069fc68d5adc5115d12) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The title of a task across days on a calendar or a week passes under the header as the page scrolls, instead of showing over the tabs, and a goal's calendar or list makes the page longer where it needs the room, instead of running out of it and scrolling the header away.

- [`ba2cd63`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/ba2cd63426b3a2e78bf8cf8c3082510f4084c726) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A person's language is kept on them from the first time the pages see them, and a plugin reads it, to write for them ([ADR-0065](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/blob/main/docs/adr/0065-a-plugin-writes-one-text-and-a-persons-language-is-kept-from-the-first-time-the-pages-see-them.md)). A person carries `language` (read as optional), in `ctx.console.people` and in `GET /api/v1/people`: the one they chose, else the one their browser asked for when they first opened the pages, `null` until then. The pages keep it with `PATCH /api/v1/me`, a custom console built with `createConsole` too, and a person changes it on the menu; a browser in another language no longer does. A browser that asks only for a language the pages do not speak leaves none. The rules' `{language}` reads the same value: an agent of a person who never chose wrote in the task's language, and writes in theirs once they have opened the pages.

- [`3776a27`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/3776a27558047a665ac0ccfe68831478e41a2522) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - `skills/building-a-plugin` starts the console on this machine with `pnpm dev:plugins --plugin <path to the built plugin>`, without the double dash: pnpm 12 hands a `--` on to the script, where the preview refused it. The preview now passes a bare `--` over, so both spellings work.

- [`06ce2e5`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/06ce2e5d53191326f949e571916fefb762933c97) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A plugin says what it requires and what it can go without: `optionalCapabilities` beside `capabilities` in the manifest. The form that enables a plugin shows the two apart, those it requires on and locked, the optional ones off until turned on, then or later, on the plugin's page, with `PATCH /api/v1/plugins/<key>/consent` and `console plugin <key> capabilities`. `ctx.plugin.capabilities` says what the plugin holds, and `updateConsent` on the plugin's client changes it. What it no longer holds is refused from its next call on, a frame's read of a file too, and a stream of events it comes to hold, or has no hook on until an upgrade, begins from then on, not with what it missed.

- [`1bd0c6c`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/1bd0c6c7d6186c5b754245da125e4bb4f4068100) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A record that names the person who did a thing names the agent they did it through (ADR-0056), as the feed always has. A decision's answer carries `agent`: the token's name when an agent wrote the answer in the person's name with `console answer`, `null` when they answered themselves; what followed carries `outcomeBy`, `outcomeAgent` and `outcomeAt`; a goal and a task carry `agent`, the agent they were set or written through. Each is read as optional, so that a console built on this package still reads an older console. The pages say "answered through <agent>" and "wrote what followed through <agent>" on a decision, "through <agent>" beside who set a goal and "Written through" on a task; the command prints `through <agent>` beside the login and `written through:` on a task; `console help answer` says that the record names the agent. The rows of the past take the agent from the feed, where it knew.

- [`63d275b`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/63d275bd8f6e1c50fb8937e50918a3f54964f572) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A plugin declares the consoles it runs on: `console` in the manifest, a semver range of the console's version as `requires` spells one (`>=0.4.3 <0.6`). A console outside the range fails the plugin when it reads it, saying the range and its own version; the range is answered as `console` on `GET /api/v1/plugins` (read as optional, for an older console), shown on the plugins' settings pages and by `console plugins`. `console-plugin init` writes it as the line of the version the plugin depends on (`--console`, the command's own unless said; none for a dependency that names no version, such as `workspace:*`), and `console-plugin check` fails a `devDependency` on `@skillcdn/console` outside it and warns of a range that leaves the command's own console out. `satisfiesRange` and its kin are exported from `@skillcdn/console/plugin`.

- [`2d88dad`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/2d88dadad8321cbd1043e9c0d9d2cf011f602839) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - Breaking: the console takes commands from 0.4.4 on (ADR-0038, ADR-0043). The event `person.manager_changed`, which the reporting line of the directory writes, is a kind an older command's reader refuses, as is `time`, the new type of a plugin's setting, in what `GET /api/v1/plugins/<key>` answers, so a console from 0.4.4 answers a command before 0.4.4 with `426` and `client.outdated`, naming the version to install (`npm install -g @skillcdn/console@latest`); the command's own floor stays at 0.4.2, since it needs nothing new of the console. The REST snapshot is taken again at the floors.

- [`01391a8`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/01391a88082a33b7af86218def488e474aa429ed) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The People page reads better: each person is a card with their role, their manager and the team they manage in rows of one shape, and the selects read as the value they hold until the pointer is on them. `PeopleList` names the manager in place of "reports to", in English and in Korean, a picture that will not load gives way to the initial in `Avatar`, and a plugin's role is granted and removed in plainer Korean words.

- [`cdca0d3`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/cdca0d3dc463a1b5026c1e2472486cf59a260894) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - `skills/building-a-plugin` says that the console started on this machine with `pnpm dev:plugins` runs the plugins' hooks and jobs in its process too, so that a decision answered there reaches the plugin's hook within seconds and a job runs at its time, as on a deployment.

- [`9d9c5ab`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/9d9c5aba8c257854e93e751d7e45f59e7ce066aa) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The directory has a reporting line: a person carries `managerId`, whom they report to, or `null` (read as optional, for an older console). An administrator sets it on the People page, with `PATCH /api/v1/people/<id>` (`managerId`, another active person who does not report to them, or `null`; `person.invalid_manager` otherwise) or with `console person manager <login> <manager login|none>`; `console people` says it beside each person; the change is the event `person.manager_changed` with `login` and `manager`, which reaches the hooks of a plugin that reads the directory. A plugin takes it as its default where it needs someone's manager, in place of a line of its own.

- [`aa770eb`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/aa770eb95696a278d920f84b88b6dc50c3b4f3f8) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The plugins' settings form draws a control per kind of setting: a `list` of an `enum` is a pill per choice (a column of boxes past eight), a list of `person` or `project` the chips of those chosen with one choice to add another, a list of anything else the items as chips with a field that adds one, `min` and `max` bounding how many; a new type `time` (`HH:MM`, read in the workspace's time zone, which the form says beside it) joins the manifest's settings; and the form is divided into the `settingGroups` a manifest declares (up to 10, each `{ key, name, description? }`), a titled block each, the settings without a `group` above them. `console plugin <key> settings --set` takes a list with commas between, as before. `GET /api/v1/plugins/<key>` answers `settingGroups` and `group` on each setting, read as optional.

- [`2b673a6`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/2b673a6a095dcaf2f354fe0533e17823566203aa) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - Breaking: the front page is one grid of tiles a person arranges by hand ([ADR-0061](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/blob/main/docs/adr/0061-the-front-page-is-one-grid-of-tiles-a-person-arranges-by-hand.md)). The projects and the plugins' widgets stand side by side on square cells, 12, 8, 6 or 4 columns by the width. A project is an `icon` of 1×1 cells or `small` of 2×2. A widget comes in sizes of one family, `small` 2×2, `medium` 4×2, `large` 4×4, `wide` 8×2 and `full` 8×4, declared as `sizes` with a `defaultSize` in the manifest, at least one of them at most four cells across, for a phone ([ADR-0062](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/blob/main/docs/adr/0062-a-widget-comes-in-a-size-a-phone-can-hold.md)), the one `size` of before reading as one of them; it fills the cells its size gives it, and its frame is told the size it has on this grid in `init.size` and in a `size` message when it changes (`frame.context.size`, `frame.onSize` in `@skillcdn/console/plugin/web`). Edit layout opens nothing on the page, and arranging reads as handling tiles: where one would land shows it faint, and those in the way slide aside from the nearest out. A tile is taken in hand with the mouse, or a finger that rests on it a moment first, and put down on any cell, those in the way moving aside; the arrow keys and Delete do the same from the keyboard, each tile carries the way off the page and the sizes it comes in, and Done keeps it all. There is no default layout: what is new takes the first room, a plugin's widgets as soon as it is on, and what was taken off stays off and is offered to add back. It is kept per width as `home` on `GET /api/v1/me` and `PATCH /api/v1/me`, read as optional. `HomeGrid` and `ProjectTile` take the place of `ProjectList` and `PluginWidgets`, in the package's exports and among the components a console is composed of. `MAX_PLUGIN_WIDGET_HEIGHT` is no longer read: the cells of a size bound a widget.

- [`cec2bcd`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/cec2bcda61b5110291e71e7536c3f7a5bbbde630) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A plugin's pages may use WebRTC where an administrator says so ([ADR-0066](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/blob/main/docs/adr/0066-webrtc-is-a-way-out-a-plugin-asks-for-and-how-a-frame-is-held-stays-in-one-place.md)). The capability `outbound:webrtc`, required or optional like any other, leaves out of the plugin's scripts the line that takes WebRTC away; no `outbound:<host>` opens it, and it opens no host for `ctx.fetch`. A plugin that lists it as optional is served its scripts uncached, so that it is gone from the next time a frame loads once it is withdrawn. A frame's `script-src` now names the plugin's version, so that a frame runs that version's scripts alone. `OUTBOUND_WEBRTC` is exported from `@skillcdn/console/plugin`.

- [`aa66599`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/aa665992c933f826de586681c4cdf533a630d0f3) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - What a plugin could not read at all ([ADR-0063](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/blob/main/docs/adr/0063-a-frame-reads-a-persons-picture-by-the-person-and-a-plugin-reads-who-it-granted-a-role.md)). `frame.people.picture(id)` in `@skillcdn/console/plugin/web` answers an address to put in an image, of a person the plugin may read: a frame loads nothing from elsewhere, so a page could show no faces. `ctx.console.people.granted(role)` answers who was granted a role of the plugin, by name, through a project or with everyone. `frame.context.workspace` says the workspace's `name` and `languages` beside its clock and its week.

## 0.4.3

### Patch Changes

- [`69a47db`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/69a47db2383d13f1f36606e700511cbba2916f1a) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A decision carries the files put on it when it is raised (ADR-0055), as a comment carries its files: `console ask --attach <path>` puts a picture of what is to be chosen between on the decision, in the one request, and `console decision` and `console task` name each file with `console file <id>`. Over REST, `POST .../decisions` takes a form whose part `decision` says what the JSON would, with a part `file` per file, at most `MAX_FILES_PER_DECISION`; a decision answers its `files`, read as optional so that an older console's decisions still read. On the pages, the decision form takes files chosen or pasted, a pasted picture placed in the context, and every card shows the pictures where the context names them, the rest after it: `DecisionCard`, `DecisionList`, `TaskThread` and `MyDecisions` take `fileHref`, `DecisionForm` and `TaskView` hand the files over with the decision, and the client's `raiseDecision` and the data's take them.

- [`1cae45e`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/1cae45edecb931926d9ce050c71152c0403f20cc) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - `skills/building-a-plugin` says how a plugin is looked at on a local console without a provider to register: in the console's checkout, `pnpm dev:plugins -- --plugin <path to the built plugin>` installs it beside the fixtures, for the administrator among the fixture's people to enable on the settings page.

- [`a406ae6`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/a406ae63e393c09d9a1a73511667da56621ef858) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - An option of a decision may carry a `key`, a lowercase word spelled as a plugin's key is and given once, which `POST .../decisions` and a plugin's `ctx.console.decisions.ask` take beside the label; the decision answers it on the option, and the `decision.answered` event names the chosen option by its label (`option`) and its key (`optionKey`), so that a program reads the answer without comparing labels. A plugin's hook receives the answer to the plugin's own decision with `board:decisions` alone. `OPTION_KEY_PATTERN` is the rule; the person's own answer carries no key; `console decision` prints a key beside its option.

- [`f39ce61`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/f39ce61387bfd9f017bab2a22a3628f451cb345c) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - Built on SkillCDN 0.4.2: every `@skillcdn` package is taken at 0.4.2. Nothing the console reads or sends changed shape; what a pack answers passes through as before, with what the packages gained: a refused read of a file that is not text in a pack read from a directory no longer tells the reader to fetch it with a GitHub credential, which only a private repository of a git host has to ask. A deployment at 0.4.2 serves a private repository in full, whatever its licenses say, and lists a pack's skills as data over REST; the console reads the first through as before and does not read the second yet.

- [`947f063`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/947f06389d69b0f8a316ac96d8b85af7e1c923bd) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - `console-plugin init` takes beside the key what a plugin bound for a repository or a registry was adapted to by hand: `--package <name>`, the package's name, a scoped one published as public; `--repository <url>` with `--directory <path>` in a repository of several plugins, which fill `repository`, `homepage` and `bugs` and the manifest's `store`; and `--languages en,ko`, the languages of the plugin's words, which gives every language map of the manifest and the translations of `SKILLCDN.md` and of the skill the other languages as English words to translate. `console-plugin check` warns of a translation left the same as English. The template names its package as `__package__`; `isLanguageMap`, `LANGUAGE_TAG`, `PACKAGE_NAME` and `sameAsEnglish` are exported with the command's modules.

- [`656dc20`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/656dc209afe9bee80f6c6fd96fdd4ba179f998f3) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The My decisions tab says how many decisions wait for the person across the projects they may see, as a project's Decisions tab says of its own: `GET /api/v1/me` answers `decisions.waiting` (`null` for nobody; read as optional, for an older console), and the pages read it again when the person raises or answers a decision, or one is raised or answered on the project's feed.

- [`ffd221b`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/ffd221bffa6b6cbfdae1155b75218bf4ed7b77aa) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A plugin with `people:read:all` hears the workspace's people events (`console:person.joined`, `person.deactivated`, `person.reactivated`, `person.role_changed`, `person.listed`, `person.unlisted`) in its hooks, with their content, as they happen, in place of comparing the directory day by day; `docs/specs/plugins.md` says so under the capability and under "Events, hooks and jobs".

- [`bf6af3b`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/bf6af3bc3ba17e18e83be339e9346c42afeaa862) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The package's `zod` is taken from 4.6.7, and the components are built against React 19.3.1; the peer range stays `^19.0.0`.

- [`2e52010`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/2e5201058d45150d5a74aeb515dd3ceeda3ff4ea) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - `skills/building-a-plugin` names the other shape a plugin's code may take: a repository of several plugins, one workspace each, published to the registry as `@<scope>/console-plugin-<key>`, since a tag installs a repository whole and carries no build; `skillcdn/console-plugins`, the reference plugin repository, is of that shape.

- [`84f0f4f`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/84f0f4f01b10da4743edbe887587272d9a6e21bf) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The plugin template, and the example held equal to it, take vitest 5.1 and `@types/node` 24.19.2, so that `console-plugin init` writes a plugin on the toolchain the console itself is built with.

## 0.4.2

### Patch Changes

- [`a3daa8f`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/a3daa8f81a42a414c73dc9f23503506a1b56b90e) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The last step of the plugins' milestone (ADR-0049, `docs/specs/plugins.md`): the skill `building-a-plugin`, the map for an agent writing a plugin, from the questions to ask and the split of a feature into commands, queries, decisions, tasks and jobs, through records or SQL, a local console with the plugin linked into `PLUGINS_DIR`, the tests and `console-plugin check`, to publishing or tagging and handing the install to the deployment as a task; "Plugins in the image" in `deploy/README.md`, how a deployment installs plugins from a lockfile with no install scripts, what `storage:sql` asks of the database user and what the two bounds mean for the container; the skills that meet plugins brought up to date (`working-the-board`, `planning-the-board`, `building-a-console`, `deploying-a-console`); and the plugin host may read a plugin's own directory where it is linked into `PLUGINS_DIR` from elsewhere, as it is while a plugin is written.

- [`5586d2b`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/5586d2bd16046310a560f0115e177d3925d3319d) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - An agent works a plugin through the command (ADR-0053, `docs/specs/plugins.md`, "The command"): `console plugins` lists the plugins one may use, `console plugin <key>` one with its commands, `console plugin <key> help <command>` one command in full, and `console plugin <key> <command> [args]` runs it, the words sent as typed to `POST /api/v1/plugins/<key>/invoke`, which parses them against the definition (`parseArgv`, from `@skillcdn/console/plugin`), `--file <path>` or `-` uploaded first and `--run <id>` naming the run the command is written on; exit `3` when the command raised a decision. `records`, `skills` and `skill` read a plugin's records and its own skills, and `enable`, `disable`, `settings`, `secret`, `grant` and `revoke` are an administrator's. The rules carry the plugins: `GET /api/v1/rules` and a project's rules answer `plugins`, which `console take` and `console rules` print under `## Plugins`. New shapes, all additions: `restPluginInvokeInputSchema`, `restPluginCommandInputSchema`, `restPluginInvocationSchema`, `restPluginFileHandleSchema`, `restPluginRecordSchema` with its page, read and versions, `restRulesPluginSchema`; `RestPluginDetail` gains `boardDefaults`; the client gains `plugins()` and `plugin(key)`. An argument of a command is not named `json`, `url`, `account`, `project`, `file` or `run` (`RESERVED_ARG_NAMES`); `PLUGIN_ENDPOINT_MS` bounds an endpoint, which a console answers at `ANY /api/v1/plugins/<key>/x/<name>` for a person or a webhook.

- [`c3ca38f`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/c3ca38f2782fa9e9d3eaa06548cb582163f17204) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A plugin reaches the board and hears of it (ADR-0052, `docs/specs/plugins.md`, "The board" and "Events, hooks and jobs"): a task, a decision and a document answer `plugin`, the key of the plugin that made them, or `null`, and the feed says "through the plugin <key>" after what a plugin did on the board (`feed.throughPlugin`); `GET /api/v1/plugins/<key>/plugin-events?after=&limit=` lists the events a plugin emitted (`restPluginEventSchema`, `restPluginEventsSchema`), for its frames. On the console's side, `ctx.console` reaches the people, the projects, the tasks, the decisions, the documents and the runs by capability and the console's policy, `ctx.events.emit` keeps a plugin's events, `ctx.fetch` makes outbound requests to the hosts consented to with the secret put in by the console, `ctx.plugins.get(key).api` calls another plugin, and hooks and jobs run from the dispatcher and the scheduler. Additions only: the floors stay.

- [`409e69f`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/409e69f8a7b90d29c86417cbe19bec94e5094802) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A plugin's frame allows forms: `PluginFrame` embeds the frame with `sandbox="allow-scripts allow-forms"`, as the frame document's own policy now says, so that a form on a plugin's page fires its `submit` event, which a browser dropped with the submission before; `form-action 'none'` in that policy keeps a submission from going anywhere, so the page handles the event itself and speaks over the bridge. Found by the browser tests the repository gained (`pnpm test:browser`), which also found that a module script is fetched with CORS from the frame's opaque origin: a console answers the bundle's files and the frames' stylesheet with `access-control-allow-origin: *`.

- [`52d0cc4`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/52d0cc44a78d009f266f747c858b8396aa7f0af9) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A plugin's pages run in a sandboxed frame the console's page embeds (ADR-0054, `docs/specs/plugins.md`, "Pages"): `PluginFrame` is the console's side of the bridge, performing a frame's calls under the plugin's own routes with the person's session and nothing else, and `PluginWidgets` the front page's grid of widgets; the tabs gain the plugins' groups (`NavItem.children`, `pluginNavItems`), a plugin with several pages its sub-tabs, and the addresses `/plugins/<key>[/<page>[/<rest>]]`, `/projects/<key>/plugins/<key>...` and `/settings/plugins[/<key>]` (`pluginHref`, `pluginSettingsHref`); the plugins' settings pages enable with consent, make or choose the board project, set the settings and the secrets, grant roles, show the events, export and purge. `CONSOLE_TOKENS` names the design tokens a frame is sent, with the new ones `--sc-color-info`, `--sc-color-neutral`, `--sc-series-1` to `-6` and `--sc-numeric`; the console's pages allow `frame-src 'self'`. The client gains `plugin(key).frameUrl`, `readFile`, `pluginEvents` and `purge`; the console's data carries `plugins` and a project's `projectPlugins`. A console answers `GET /api/v1/plugins/<key>/frame?page=|widget=`, `GET .../web/<version>/<path>` and `GET /api/v1/plugin-runtime/console.css`.

- [`556d86b`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/556d86bdc0b38ddd5bed4df5a04dad49301a2290) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - `plugin.purged` is a kind of event: an administrator purged a plugin, and everything the console kept of it is gone; the feed says so in English and Korean. `RestPluginPurgeInput` (`{ confirm: "<key>" }`) is what `POST /api/v1/plugins/<key>/purge` is sent.

- [`f6aff72`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/f6aff72dc752c3d63a12f35dc3c1c9630ae28dd4) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - `building-a-console` and `deploying-a-console`, read against the authoring guide and dry-read by a fresh agent. The building skill keeps its configuration in `references/configuring.md`, installs the package at the version the deployment pins before it plans, asks where the deployment repository is, and commits in it as its agreement says without pushing, since a push there deploys. The deploying skill makes the zone before the plan, since it costs nothing and its nameservers must reach the person early, checks what the scan of a live domain brought over before the switch, carries the section on the organization's own console its phase 4 named, asks for the organization's name and the people's place in plain words, and names the settings of a shared console among what a second run changes.

- [`d2351be`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/d2351be32fc67a2916c6831184518b26e223b88f) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A card held near the board's edge carries the board on. While a card is dragged, the board scrolls on every frame the pointer is within the edge, with a mouse or a finger, still or moving, the nearer the edge the faster, as an icon held at the edge of a phone's screen turns the page; before, the board moved only as the pointer did, so that a card held still at the edge went nowhere. `edgeScrollOf` meters it and is exported from the board's module for a board of your own.

- [`6fdedd0`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/6fdedd025c303e568d109b0d231bc66f1c98c055) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The skills the package carries, read again against the authoring guide of the reference pack. `planning-the-board` is new, for the work a person puts on the board and the project they set up: tasks written from what they say or plan, with their days, categories, goals, parents and workers; goals on the roadmap; a project made or changed, with its members and its rules; the workspace's categories, people and settings; a private pack connected. It is split from `working-the-board`, which works one task to its end, reads the task in full before taking it, names the quotas and the refusal of a person who is out where a run meets them, and reads the project's pack as its reference `the-pack.md` says. Both descriptions say what comes out, then when to use the skill in the words people say, then which neighbouring skill to use instead; the manifest says what an instruction is and what is material.

- [`a06f722`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/a06f7226f6bf519612cde6e57b72a337c54972f4) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - Breaking: the console holds its plugins (ADR-0049, ADR-0050, `docs/specs/plugins.md`): `GET /api/v1/plugins` lists them with their state and the asker's roles, `GET /api/v1/plugins/<key>` is one with its commands, queries, settings and, to an administrator, its consent, projects and grants, and an administrator enables (`POST .../enable`, with consent to every capability, the board project to make or choose, the projects, the settings), disables, configures (`PATCH .../settings`), seals secrets (`PUT`/`DELETE .../secrets/<name>`) and grants roles (`GET`/`POST .../grants`, `DELETE .../grants/<id>`); the shapes are `restPluginSchema`, `restPluginsSchema`, `restPluginDetailSchema`, `restPluginGrantSchema` and the inputs, the routes `REST_ROUTES.plugins` and `pluginPath`, the states `PLUGIN_STATES`. Every event names the plugin that caused it in `plugin` (`null` for the console's own), and eight kinds are new: `plugin.enabled`, `plugin.disabled`, `plugin.configured`, `plugin.granted`, `plugin.revoked`, `plugin.upgraded`, `plugin.command` and `plugin.failed`, with their words in the feed in English and Korean; `plugin.failed` is also a refusal (500), what a plugin's code throws that is not its own refusal. The floors move to 0.4.2 on both sides (ADR-0038, ADR-0043): an older command would refuse an event of a kind it does not know, so a console from 0.4.2 takes commands from 0.4.2, and this command needs a console from 0.4.2, which answers `plugin` on every event; the REST snapshot is taken again at them.

- [`4eaa358`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/4eaa3589728641569c6178afb7a617c9ebd7521a) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The deploying skill reads in two pages of `load_skill` with room to grow: what a later run changes (a new version, a new connector, the next size, a member, a secret, a shared console, a pause or removal) is `references/running-it-again.md`, read when such a run comes, and the terms are the few the body does not define itself.

- [`08059bf`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/08059bf2249b2bb045d8550a1abd47b905f376b1) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The contract of a plugin is in the package, the first step of the plugin system (ADR-0049 to ADR-0054, `docs/specs/plugins.md`): `@skillcdn/console/plugin` with the manifest's schema (`parsePluginManifest`, `RESERVED_PLUGIN_KEYS`, the capabilities and their words, the bounds), `definePlugin` and its definers (`defineCollection`, `defineCommand`, `defineQuery`, `defineEndpoint`, `arg.*`), the types of `ctx` and `describePlugin`, which yields the definition as data; `@skillcdn/console/plugin/web` with `connect()`, the frame's side of the bridge, and `@skillcdn/console/plugin/web/react` with `usePlugin()`; `@skillcdn/console/plugin/testing` with `createTestContext()`, records in memory for a plugin's own tests; and the second bin, `console-plugin`, whose `init` writes a plugin from the template the package ships and whose `check` reads a plugin as a console would, the server module loaded under the host's hardening. No console runs a plugin yet: the host, the routes and the pages follow.

- [`5a411b0`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/5a411b06a5d6341a45583f6919ff026a0e7151f2) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The plugins' pages sit in the console as its own do. A frame document paints no background and is as tall as its content (`html[data-console]` in the stylesheet), so the box the console draws shows through and a widget is as tall as what it shows; the widgets above the projects keep only the page head's distance, and those below the projects their own (`sc-widgets-top`, `sc-widgets-bottom`). A frame reads in the console's own faces: the document links `GET /api/v1/plugin-runtime/fonts.css`, the `@font-face` rules of the console's build with their files at `GET /api/v1/plugin-runtime/assets/<file>`. The Settings page lists the plugins with their state for an administrator, each leading to its own page, where it offered a button and a paragraph; the plugins' own list page has no paragraph either (`plugins.settings.intro` is gone from the packs). The plugins' tabs and the organization's own come before Settings, which is last, at the workspace and in a project; the Settings tab is current on the plugins' settings pages. The template's widget, and the example's, pad nothing of their own, since the console's box does.

- [`f00d1e1`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/f00d1e16401cce0a0db1421224fb3a6647fdc737) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A plugin's settings page reads at a glance, with as few words as it takes. Its name, state, key and version are in the head, with the way to disable it; what it is on and holds is a row each (its board project, its projects, the capabilities it holds, its commands and queries, what agents read of it), where the facts stood in a grid that broke. Enabling is one form in three parts: the capabilities, each by a name in the person's language with one sentence of what it opens (`plugins.capability` in the packs), ticked one by one, those consented to before ticked already; the board project; the projects. A secret shows when it was set, with its field and buttons on one line. The roles are the plugin's own, each with what it is for and who holds it, every member for the default role (`defaultRole`, which `GET /api/v1/plugins/<key>` now names), each grant a chip with the way to revoke it, and one choice to grant it to a person, the members of a project or everyone. Export and purge are two rows of one panel, the purge's field offered once the plugin is disabled. The paragraphs that explained the capabilities, the roles and the purge are gone; `PluginSettingsView` takes the workspace's `people`. A `person` or `project` setting is chosen among the workspace's by name and held as its id, where the form took an id typed by hand. The Korean pack says 활성 and 비활성 of a plugin, and 활성화 and 비활성화 of the ways, as it does of people, where it said 켜짐, 꺼짐, 켜기 and 끄기.

- [`67bb4f4`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/67bb4f4d6459ed647f42115d353a2195de5ecdfc) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The Settings page says which version the console is. The first row of the workspace's settings names it, with the range of command versions it takes, as `GET /api/v1/me` answers them in `console` (ADR-0025), so that whoever keeps the agents' commands up to date reads there what they must match; `WorkspaceSettingsView` takes them as `console`, and says nothing when the console is one from before the answer. Both packs carry the words.

## 0.4.1

### Patch Changes

- [`32d0abb`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/32d0abb19d24f80a7a88bc75712d4961856da426) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - On the People page, what let a person in is said by its name: a person a Workspace domain let in reads "Account at acme.test", from the domain of their login, where the row read "Of the domain"; a person the organization let in reads "Member of acme" as before, and the plain words stand only where the console names no organization. Both packs carry the new word.

- [`261896f`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/261896f696465d84a3213570388a9265640400dd) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - On the People page, a person the organization at the git host let in is said to be a member of it by the organization's name ("Member of acme"), set apart from their own login with a middle dot, where the row read "login Of the organization" and, in Korean, as if the login were the organization's name. The English and Korean packs carry the new word.

- [`37b5c01`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/37b5c013f893ed60fac851e98eb15a27e0755ef0) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - Breaking: the pages load no picture from elsewhere. A picture named in Markdown over https, and a person's picture from the git host, are read through the console at `GET /api/v1/pictures?src=` (ADR-0048), which fetches it from a public address only, keeps it in memory a while and tells the browser to keep it a day, so that no origin learns who looks at a page; the content security policy a build is served under is `img-src 'self' data:`, so a custom console's own pages load their pictures from the build or through the same route. `Markdown` and `Avatar` read the address through the new `RemotePicturesContext`, which `createConsole` provides from the client's new `pictureUrl`; a component outside a composition reads it at the API's own path. `REST_ROUTES.pictures` names the route.

- [`d73938b`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/d73938bea8a2ebea44b549ec9a98e5d601831d5a) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - Breaking: a project is made by an administrator, on the console's own pages or through their agent, and they become its owner; a member's `POST /api/v1/projects`, and `console project new` on a member's token, are refused with `auth.forbidden`, where before any member made one. The Projects page shows the form, and the way to it, to an administrator alone, and the empty page tells a member whom to ask (`projects.empty.bodyMember` in both packs).

- [`ffb4cb4`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/ffb4cb4012c71c6aa490b51f6ab22701eed01ef0) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The command says why a token is refused when its person is out. A console from 0.4.1 answers `auth.deactivated`, for a person deactivated, or `auth.not_admitted`, for one whose domain or organization is no longer named, in place of `auth.required` for a token it knows, on every route and at `GET /api/v1/me`; the token is kept and works again when the person is back in. The command prints the two with what to do, `console logout` says that the console keeps such a token and forgets it on the machine, and `console help` lists them among the refusals any command may meet.

- [`b931598`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/b9315986e9a9636fdfb5ce1af76028608f5b5d54) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - Built on SkillCDN 0.4.1: every `@skillcdn` package is taken at 0.4.1. Nothing the console reads or sends changed shape; what the packages gained, a pack reads through: the manifest of a folder named where `console skills <folder>` lists it, the pictures and the originals of a document on its outline, the README of a folder marked as its overview among search results, and a file that is not text answered with where it opens, as the `skillcdn` command answers it.

- [`d984533`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/d984533a235ceee6b6c4987a7784bd46183a373d) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The console bounds what a workspace, a project, a task and a person hold at once, where a runaway script could grow them without end: `MAX_PROJECTS_PER_WORKSPACE` (500), `MAX_TASKS_PER_PROJECT` (20,000), `MAX_GOALS_PER_PROJECT` (1,000), `MAX_DECISIONS_PER_PROJECT` (20,000), `MAX_DOCUMENTS_PER_PROJECT` (5,000) and `MAX_RUNS_PER_TASK` (500), those put away counted, refused as `project.too_many`, `task.too_many`, `goal.too_many`, `decision.too_many`, `document.too_many` and `run.too_many` (`409`); and `MAX_STREAMS_PER_PERSON` (20) streams of the live feed for one person with `MAX_STREAMS` (1,000) for a process, refused as `feed.too_many` (`429`, with `retry-after`). The command's help names the bounds and the refusals, and says what to do when one is met.

- [`a8c3c94`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/a8c3c94943a972a436ec801471abb07827b4a9cf) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - Breaking: the console claims a pack token at the deployment on `POST .../pack-token/connect/claim`, for a project's token and for the workspace's, and `GET .../pack-token/connect` reads how the connection stands and changes nothing, where before the read itself claimed the token and kept it, so that a link followed from anywhere, carrying the session, did so outside the page (ADR-0046). `console project pack-token --connect` and `console workspace pack-token --connect` ask the claim at the interval the deployment named, and so do the settings pages; `PackConnect` and `PackTokenWays` take `claim` beside `poll`, and the client has `claimPackTokenConnection` and `claimWorkspacePackTokenConnection`. The floors move to 0.4.1 on both sides (ADR-0038): this command needs a console from 0.4.1, whose `--connect` it would otherwise wait on for a claim nobody makes, and a console from 0.4.1 takes commands from 0.4.1 for the same reason.

- [`8b2b33d`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/8b2b33d6003e8228d25a1b6d60f3fdff770103ae) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A board with a long history opens without reading its whole feed first. The pages read the newest page of a project's feed in one request (`GET .../events?latest=true`, which a console from 0.4.1 answers with the newest `limit` events, oldest first, and whether there are earlier ones; `EventFilter.latest` on the client), where before they read the feed from the beginning a hundred events at a time, one round trip after another, and showed the board only then: on a project with thousands of events, seconds over a slow path. The stream goes on from the newest event as before.

- [`91abdad`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/91abdad06871bd6023d4b59d48a53c62f3cf743b) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The files of one project may be bounded in bytes together, where the deployment says so: `PROJECT_FILE_BYTES_AT_MOST` counts the files runs hand in, those put in comments and those attached to pages, each by its size, and a file that would take the project over it is refused as `project.too_many_bytes` (`409`) before any byte is kept. No bound unless set. The command's help names the refusal on `hand-in`, `comment` and `attach`, and says what to do: hand in a link instead.

- [`f6e3a8a`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/f6e3a8adab6c7471c4a8fda8781eb35195d02ae4) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The sign-in page says the way in. Each button reads "Sign in with GitHub" (or Google), carries the provider's mark before its words, is wide and a step taller than a control, keeps the focus ring at its own radius, and from the click until the browser leaves says "Going to GitHub…" with a ring turning in its text color, the other buttons set aside; a page the browser brings back from its cache is itself again. Under each button, a line says who gets in through it, from the new `admits` of every provider `GET /api/v1/me` answers: the organization at the git host whose members are in, the Workspace domains whose accounts are, or the people an administrator added. `SignIn` reads `admits` as optional, so a console built on this package shows an older console's page without the line. The word `signIn.continueWith` of the packs is `signIn.signInWith` now, with `signIn.going` and `signIn.admits` beside it.

## 0.4.0

### Minor Changes

- [`d7a2da4`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/d7a2da447c532df0b5faca5fbf000207fc61e4c6) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - Breaking: the console is built on SkillCDN 0.4 (ADR-0034): every `@skillcdn` package is taken at 0.4.0, and the version moves to the 0.4 line. The command and the console go together within a line (ADR-0038), so the floors are 0.4.0 on both sides: a 0.4 console refuses a 0.3 command with `426` and `client.outdated`, naming the version to install (`npm install -g @skillcdn/console@0.4.0`), and a 0.4 command refuses a 0.3 console as `console.outdated`; a deployment of this release has every agent's command updated once. Nothing the console reads or sends changed shape otherwise.
  
  What SkillCDN 0.4 brings to a pack read through the console flows through as every answer of a pack does: a search names the sections of a document that matched, each with the offset and limit that read it (`sections` on `RestFind`); a file's path browses to the file's outline (`outline` on `RestBrowse`, with `entries` empty); and a result says how it was ranked where the deployment searches by meaning as well as by words (`ranking`). The connection instructions `console skills` prints say the same, as the deployment says them.

### Patch Changes

- [`dc0c4d3`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/dc0c4d340bb992106b2fe9b8da93c5c506dd8350) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A phone's pages as one design. Every page's head is laid out the same way, the name over a word about it over the ways the page offers as a row of equal buttons, with one padding and radius for the panes. The board scrolls edge to edge, its columns nearly as wide as the window, and the tabs snap tab by tab; the board's filters stay on one line and scroll sideways. A task's head is stacked, its parent on one line and its ways as equal buttons, its facts one to a row. The month is clipped at its edge, its bars reach exactly over the narrower gap, "N more" on a day keeps to one line, and a day shows a dot per task in rows under its number, a task planned across days a dot of its category's colour on each of them, the day growing to hold them; the number covers the whole day, so that a finger anywhere on it lists the day's tasks. The roadmap's months read as the year over the month, a goal card's span of days stands on a line of its own, and the goal page says its progress once, under the bar. The workload table's headings wrap at spaces and its cells are tighter, and a skill's card is never wider than the window.

- [`dc0c4d3`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/dc0c4d340bb992106b2fe9b8da93c5c506dd8350) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - Every badge is a pill, a tone says one thing, and a badge's kind shows in how it is filled.
  
  - `Badge` takes `kind` (`state`, `priority`, `due` or `count`, which the state, priority, due and count badges set as `sc-badge-kind-*`) and a `className` of its own, for a step within a kind.
  - A tone: neutral for what merely is, accent (blue) for what is at work, success (green) for what is done, warning (amber) for what wants attention within the week, point (orange) for what presses today or tomorrow, danger (red) for what is late or has failed. An administrator and an owner are marked in the accent, not the warm point.
  - A kind: a plain badge (a role, a count, public or private) is a soft fill with a line of its hue; a category and a state a soft fill with no line; a priority a soft fill with dark words of its hue, low a grey, high an orange, urgent a red; a due day a tag with a line, a very light fill and bold figures lined up, all in the hue of how near the day is: grey for a day set or a task that is over, amber within the week, orange tomorrow, a deeper orange today (`sc-badge-due-today`), red once past.
  - Words on a yellow fill are a warm charcoal, since a dark yellow reads as a muddy brown. Each fill is deep enough to stand off the sheet on its own, and every pair of words reads above 4.5:1 on its fill. A badge's words are centred on their cap height and baseline whatever script they are in, where the browser fits a line box to the glyphs (`text-box: trim-both cap alphabetic`); where it cannot, the lift by language stands.
  - The eight category colours are spread around the wheel, each fill its own hue at a glance with its words dark in the same hue, and each has a mark colour, a clear mid-tone of its hue (`--sc-category-mark`, new), for the legend dots, the goal flags, the swatches and the edges and progress of bars on the month and the roadmap, where the dark line colour made for words went muddy and alike. A console that sets only the line and the fill gets its line where the mark would be.

- [`dc0c4d3`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/dc0c4d340bb992106b2fe9b8da93c5c506dd8350) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - Every page is laid out alike, uses the page, and reads at a glance.
  
  - The header and the tabs span the window on every page, so that the mark, the tabs and the person's menu stay where they are between the board and the other pages. Every page keeps the same margin at its sides, 3rem (`--sc-gutter`, new; as before on a narrow screen), and starts at the same height under the header, a page's head aligning its title and its ways on the baseline; the reading pages start where the board starts, their text still held to `--sc-width-prose`; the board page keeps as much room below its columns as above them. The room for a scrollbar is kept on every page (`scrollbar-gutter: stable`), so that the header does not shift between a page that scrolls and one that does not.
  - A section's title runs a line to the end, a page's head and a task's head are parted from what follows by a hairline, and what was on one line stands on its own: a project reads as its mark and name, its key under them and its badges on a line of their own, with what waits in it under a line at the foot, the mark hanging in the margin where the rest wraps; a task in a list has its project under its title and its badges at the end, the people at work on it staying at the end of the row when the badges wrap; a run's head says the agent and its state on one line, with the way to end it at the end, and who it is for and since when on the next, a report's hour heading it small; a comment's hour stands at the end of its line; the task a decision is about is a chip; a goal's title stands alone over its badges and days; the facts beside a task stand in one column with a hairline between each; a project's skills are cards two abreast, each with its name, its folder, its words and its address; a goal's facts are cells with the name over the value; a task's subtasks and links, and what a run handed in, are rows on sheets, the state of a subtask at the end of its row; a page in a list reads on two lines, its title and under it its path, version, writer and time; the versions of a page are rows with the number, the title and who wrote it when; who wrote a page is labelled without a colon.
  - The feed takes the page's width; the decisions put those waiting and the record side by side where the window is 64rem or wider; the roadmap's goals are cards two or more to a row; under the wide layout a task reads its text first, then its facts and the ways to its forms, then the discussion and what happened.
  - On a card of the board the task's number stands on a line of its own above the title, small and in the code face; a column is headed at the reading size rather than in small capitals, and its count is a quiet grey pill on the column.
  - The people of the organization and the members of a project are cards in a grid (`.sc-people`, columns of at least 18rem; the markup is as it was): the person on the first line, with "(you)" beside the name (`.sc-person-you`) rather than within the account line, the account and where it came from on the second, what they are and the ways to their work on the third, the member's role line small. In the header the live dot sits by the person's menu, whose picture is a little larger.
  - The facts on a settings page, a project's and the workspace's, read as a table: a row per fact with its name on the left and its value beside it, a hairline between rows, the name over the value where the window is narrow.

- [`bb2c96e`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/bb2c96eff788ce209870230d3f19f15e52c119a4) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The board is narrowed by words as they are typed: a field at the head of the filters, with no button, keeps the cards whose title, number, owner, workers or category contain every word given, whatever the case, and the count says how many it keeps. The words are in the board's address as the other filters are (`q=`), so that a narrowed board is a link; `BoardFilter` carries them as `words`, `filterTasks` reads them, and `boardHref` writes them.

- [`dc0c4d3`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/dc0c4d340bb992106b2fe9b8da93c5c506dd8350) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The calendar's month, who has what, and the roadmap's months.
  
  - A day's number on the month is a button: pressed, the day's tasks, those on it and those lying across it, are listed under the month with their badges (the words `calendar.onDay`, `calendar.nothingOnDay`, `calendar.close`), and pressed again or closed, the list goes. A day with more than four tasks of its own shows three and the way to the rest (`.sc-calendar-more`, the words `calendar.more` and `calendar.fewer`), so that a busy week is as tall as the others until a day is opened.
  - Who has what, under the month, is a table on a sheet of its own, titled "담당자별 작업 현황" in Korean: a quiet band for the headings, a hairline between people, the numbers in columns of their own on the right in the display size, the one due this week and the one late with a dot of their colour, and the row under the pointer lit a little. It sits on the left under what is late, and what has no day yet runs down the right, so that the page under the month fills evenly.
  - A bar of a task that is over is paler in its own colour instead of see-through, so that every piece of it is one shade whatever lies under it, and the card of the task under the pointer is solid on the raised white.
  - A goal's label on the roadmap's months keeps to one line beside its bar, so that every row is as tall as its bar and the labels line up with the bars: a long title is cut short and said in full on hover (`title`), and the goal's state stays beside it or, on a narrow screen, is left to its card below the months.

- [`d1b5a28`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/d1b5a28654c7b645d721f7c5100d4496038fe2ad) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A card of the board is compact again, since a column holds many: the line under its title is gone, and its vertical room and the gap before its badges are smaller. A badge is a lower pill everywhere, a hair of room above and below its word and a little more at its sides, where it had grown tall with the renewal; the people at the end of the row are as they were, so that a due day reads at a glance as before. Styles only.

- [`af0cddc`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/af0cddc3ea7420f1291295942888ce14dceffb97) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The command reads what SkillCDN 0.4 names: `console skills <file>` prints a document's outline, every heading with its level and the offset and limit that read its section, its pages joined (`--json` joins them too); `console skill --file <path> --offset <n> --limit <n>` reads one page of the file as asked, the section a search or an outline named, and says where the file continues rather than asking for the rest (`--offset` 0 or more, `--limit` 1 to 100,000, both with `--file` only); `console skills --search` prints the sections of a document that matched, and how the page was ranked where the deployment searches by meaning. `console help skills` and `console help skill` say so, and the skill working-the-board (1.22) names the two reads.

- [`dc0c4d3`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/dc0c4d340bb992106b2fe9b8da93c5c506dd8350) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The controls: a select (`select.sc-input`) draws a thin chevron like the browser's own, as far from its right edge as its words are from the left, and takes the room for it on its right; the chevron is a picture held in a new token, `--sc-select-chevron`, a grey stroke that reads on a light field and a dark one, which a console of its own may replace. A file field is as tall as any other field, with a button of the console's own to choose the file and the chosen file's name beside it, nothing of the browser's own file field seen; a page's attach form puts the file across, the name to call it under that and the attach button across the bottom. The ways under a comment's field stand at one height.

- [`8582416`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/85824163b38d98a03dc8b5c87dc33eb3f0a9dc83) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A decision is addressed to a person (ADR-0042): whoever raises it, or the person an agent asks for, unless another person of the project is named, and the decision carries them as `decider`. My decisions, a page beside My work (`/decisions`), lists the decisions addressed to the person across their projects, the ones that wait first and then the answered as the record, and those the person asked of someone else, every row naming who asked and who is to answer, a page at a time (`MyDecisions`, `client.myDecisions`, `raised`); My work's decisions are those waiting for the person's own answer. `DecisionForm` takes `deciders`, the people who may be named, found by typing, and `me`, the default; a decision card says whom it is for where that is not the one who asked, and a waiting decision says whom it waits for. `console ask --to <login|me>` names the decider and `console decisions --mine` and `--raised` list one's own across the projects; `console work` says the decisions waiting for you. The REST API takes `deciderId` on a decision raised (`decision.invalid_decider` for a person who may not work in the project) and answers `GET /api/v1/decisions?open=&raised=&limit=&offset=`.

- [`881f31a`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/881f31a965443c22f8932f8bdd1982fe78a974d5) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A choice made elsewhere reaches the record: `console answer <id> --option <n>|--own "<answer>" [--note <markdown>|--file <path>]` writes a person's answer on a decision in their name through the agent, as they gave it in chat, in a meeting or by changing the task, with where and when in the note, so that the board shows as waiting only what is still open and a run that waited goes on. The skill working-the-board (1.25), its terms and the console's own rules say what a decision is, a record of a choice that is a person's to make wherever they make it, that an answer given elsewhere is written there before the work goes on, and that a briefing starts from the decisions waiting for the person's answer.

- [`538c51d`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/538c51d25f94936d291df0cdd0d7f91e82a4f923) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - Breaking: `DecisionForm` finds the task a decision is about by searching the board a page at a time (ADR-0037): it takes `findTasks`, the search the task form takes, in place of `tasks`, every task of the board; given `taskId`, raised from a task's page, it is about that task and shows no field, as before. The words `decisions.form.noTask` stand in the empty search field.

- [`e689237`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/e6892376eec6a293ee333cda39092b84d973b249) Thanks [@samo-mozzi](https://github.com/samo-mozzi)! - Breaking: the feed reads by days, each person named once for a run of their events.
  
  - `EventFeed` heads the events by the day they fell on (`.sc-feed-day`, `.sc-feed-day-title`), and within a day groups a run of events by the same person as the same agent under one name (`.sc-feed-run`, `.sc-feed-actor`), each row (`.sc-feed-item`) the hour (`.sc-feed-clock`, 24-hour, the full instant in its title) and the sentence (`.sc-feed-words`). The rows are parted by hairlines rather than boxed, and the sentence reads as text, underlined under the pointer.
  - The markup changes: the list is a `section.sc-feed` of day sections, each an `ol.sc-feed-day-list` of runs, each an `ol.sc-feed-run-list` of rows; a row no longer holds the person or a `Time`. A stylesheet that boxed `.sc-feed-item` or placed its three columns styles the run and the row instead; `.sc-feed-item` is no longer a sheet.

- [`a6ce839`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/a6ce839887848e916ba8f3ba598f0e281a29376b) Thanks [@samo-mozzi](https://github.com/samo-mozzi)! - The brand lockup in the header keeps the proportions of the brand's lockup file: the wordmark's height and the room between it and the symbol follow the symbol at the file's ratios (new tokens `--sc-brand-wordmark-ratio`, `--sc-brand-gap-ratio`), and the symbol is a step bigger so that the wordmark keeps its size.

- [`dc0c4d3`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/dc0c4d340bb992106b2fe9b8da93c5c506dd8350) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The light look, as it settled: one field, panes of glass over it, white sheets inside them, and fewer visual elements.
  
  - The field is one gradient from the top (`--sc-field-gradient`, new, a pale grey-blue falling to white), painted by the shell over `--sc-color-bg` and nothing else; the lights drawn over it before are gone (the tokens merged, below).
  - A pane is translucent white (`--sc-color-glass` at 60%), blurred and saturated behind (`--sc-glass-backdrop`), with light falling down its face from the top edge (`--sc-glass-highlight`, new), a rim one pixel wide in a soft white (`--sc-color-glass-edge`, its border), a sheen along its top edge (`--sc-glass-sheen`) and a soft lift (`--sc-glass-lift`). The columns of the board are panes without the rim and the shadow, so that no line runs down their sides and the gaps between them stay as light as the field; the header lets the field through; the bar and the picture overlay blur as the panes do.
  - A sheet, a card or a row inside a pane, is a lightly lifted white tile: 82% white (`--sc-color-sheet`), white under the pointer (`--sc-color-sheet-hover`), with a light edge (`--sc-border`) and a little lift. A pane and a sheet are the two surfaces.
