  - Four shadows: the stage, the pane, the glow and the focus ring; the rings and glows of the live dot, of a waiting decision and of the sign-in mark are gone, and the primary button's hover is the same glow. Two borders as tokens: `--sc-border` and `--sc-border-strong` (new, for what is typed into); the marking line of a quote, an answer and a report is two pixels of the accent; the callout, the sign-in mark and a chosen pick are on the plain one. A hairline (`--sc-hairline`, new) parts things inside a pane. Two opacities: hidden, and faded at 0.55 for everything that is over or put away. A scrollbar's thumb is a light grey (`--sc-scrollbar-thumb`, new, the control edge at a little under half) on a clear track, the page's, the board's and a column's.
  - `--sc-color-success` is a shade darker, so that it reads at 4.5:1 where the field is deepest.

- [`dc9c61e`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/dc9c61ee796952c0f0730cd7f8b2d97a02f373c2) Thanks [@samo-mozzi](https://github.com/samo-mozzi)! - Breaking: the pages are light by default (ADR-0040). `console.css` sets `color-scheme: light`, and every colour token takes a light value that keeps SkillCDN's branding: the ground `--sc-color-bg` #f7f8fc with white above it, the text #14141a, the accent #2c5bc4 on a solid #3b6fd4, the point, the tones and the categories' lines dark enough to read on their pale fills, the field a pale sky with the brand's blue at the top, and the glass a frosted white. No markup changes. The default console shows the black wordmark of `@skillcdn/brand`, and the skill building-a-console (1.6) maps a website onto light pages.

- [`bfdc4c1`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/bfdc4c19ac5aad770ce8555dfce924b84605b721) Thanks [@samo-mozzi](https://github.com/samo-mozzi)! - Breaking: the board folds the columns of finished work, and a task's ways to its forms stand in its head.
  
  - `Board` folds the columns `folded` names, `FOLDED_STATES`, done and dropped, by default, so that the work at hand has the room, and `folded={[]}` folds none. A folded column is its header alone, a small box at the top of the board with its name, its count and the way to open it (`.sc-column-folded`, `.sc-column-toggle`; the words `board.fold` and `board.unfold`), and still takes a card dragged onto it. The board lays its columns out with flex rather than grid. Where a column holds more cards than it shows, the last card in view fades at the foot of the column rather than being cut by its edge, and the column's scrollbar shows only while the pointer is over the column or something in it has the focus.
  - `TaskView` puts the ways to its forms (edit, raise a decision, put away, bring back), `.sc-task-actions`, in its head beside the title, where they were at the foot of its facts; the title, its parent and its badges are in `.sc-task-heading` within `.sc-task-header`. A stylesheet that placed `.sc-task-aside .sc-task-actions` places `.sc-task-header > .sc-task-actions`.
  - On a narrow screen the tabs stay on one line and scroll sideways, the edge fading where more of them are, and `Shell` brings the current tab into view.

- [`8b6721c`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/8b6721c8e51023ecf50b6b77da57f6700674b18f) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The feed says when the console ended a run that had gone quiet (ADR-0041): a run open, not waiting for an answer and not heard from for twelve hours (what the deployment sets) is ended as abandoned by the console itself, `run.ended` naming no actor and the reason, and the feed reads it as the console's doing, not the agent's. The skill working-the-board (1.23) tells an agent so: report at the milestones, wait on a decision with `--wait` or come back for it, and take the task again when a run was ended that way.

- [`e39a675`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/e39a675bca63a05516b78628cfbc5feb51a06296) Thanks [@samo-mozzi](https://github.com/samo-mozzi)! - The stylesheet reads only the tokens it defines, so that a stylesheet of your own reaches every place they are used: the roadmap's title is `--sc-text-md`, where it named `--sc-text-base`, which no rule defines, and the focus of a picture, of a card's menu button and the column a card is dragged over are drawn with `--sc-focus-ring`, where they spelled the ring out. Nothing changes in the default look. A test of the package holds both.

- [`548cfdc`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/548cfdc1e0b8feac7b58ff4da7af150905ce869a) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - Breaking: the tokens merged, 115 to 86, every one of them read by a rule, so that re-skinning has fewer knobs and none that does nothing. A stylesheet of your own that set one of the tokens gone sets what took its place:
  
  - The field has no lights: `--sc-color-sky`, `--sc-color-far`, `--sc-color-far-turn`, `--sc-color-vignette`, `--sc-color-light-core`, `-body`, `-mid` and `-halo`, `--sc-light-strength`, `-top`, `-height`, `-inset` and `-blur` are gone with the rules that drew them; the shell paints `--sc-field-gradient` over `--sc-color-bg`, and nothing else.
  - A pane's rim is its border, `1px solid var(--sc-color-glass-edge)`: `--sc-glass-lens`, `--sc-glass-lens-width` and `--sc-glass-lens-mask` are gone with the pseudo-element that drew the rim. A sheet reads `--sc-border` and a fixed lift, and has no blur of its own: `--sc-sheet-edge`, `--sc-sheet-lift` and `--sc-sheet-backdrop` are gone.
  - What is under the pointer is mixed from the accent where it is drawn: `--sc-color-accent-hover` and `--sc-color-accent-solid-hover` are gone, and so is `--sc-color-selection`, mixed from `--sc-color-accent-solid` at `::selection`.
  - `--sc-color-bg-subtle` is gone (`--sc-color-bg-inset` where it was read), and `--sc-color-field` too (`--sc-color-bg-raised`, which it was).
  - The marking line of a quote, an answer and a report is `2px solid var(--sc-color-accent)` where it is drawn: `--sc-border-accent` is gone, `--sc-focus-ring` staying for the focus.
  - One lift for a label, `--sc-label-lift`, set again under `:root:lang(ko)`: `--sc-label-lift-hangul` and `--sc-label-lift-text` are gone.
  - `--sc-glow-bright` and `--sc-width-page`, which no rule read, are gone.
  - `--sc-color-text-faint` is #666c7c, a step lighter than `--sc-color-text-muted` (1.44:1 between them, where they were 1.10:1 apart) and 4.6:1 on the deepest ground.
  - The small capitals over a group are one rule, `.sc-eyebrow`, which the menu headings, the field labels, the facts' names, the feed's days, the decision labels and the tables' heads share; the class is for a label of your own. `LinkButton`, a link that reads as a button, is exported.
  - Not tokens, by decision: the control sizes, the blur, the opacities, the z-indexes and the breakpoints stay in the stylesheet. The dark look of 0.3.2 is not kept as a stylesheet; a theme is a setting to come.
  
  The package's README lists the 86 by family.

- [`dc0c4d3`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/dc0c4d340bb992106b2fe9b8da93c5c506dd8350) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The pages read a step larger, with a clearer hierarchy, every size and corner from the scale.
  
  - `--sc-text-xs` is 13px and `--sc-text-sm` 15px; the snug leading is a token (`--sc-leading-snug`, new, 1.45), and so is the tracking (`--sc-tracking-tight` for titles, `--sc-tracking-wide` for the small labels over a group, `--sc-tracking-code` for a code shown in groups, all new), every rule reading them. On a page in Korean the tight and the wide tracking are off, and a label is lifted less in its box (`--sc-label-lift` set again under `:root:lang(ko)`; the pages read `<html lang>`), since Hangul sits higher in its box than Latin capitals; menu links read the same.
  - `--sc-color-text-muted` is #4b5563 and `--sc-color-text-faint` #666c7c, a step apart and both above 4.5:1 on the deepest ground.
  - The text of a task and of a page of the documents is read at `--sc-text-md`; a card's number is `--sc-text-xs` and a task's beside its title `--sc-text-xl`, where they were off the scale; the answer a decision took reads in the text colour, marked by a green dot before it, where the whole of it was green; a person's picture is round, and every corner reads a radius token (the sign-in mark `--sc-radius-xl`, the dots `--sc-radius-full`); a secondary button is raised white with a light edge (`--sc-color-border`, a little lift), the strong edge under the pointer, where it was outlined in the strong edge.

## 0.3.2

### Patch Changes

- [`5dd89ff`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/5dd89ffc6067d53d8deaefe07eac330a36c9e6d1) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - Breaking: a task has any number of workers in place of one assignee (ADR-0037). On the wire a task carries `workers`, the people at work on it in the order they came to it, instead of `assignee`; `POST` and `PATCH .../tasks` take `workerIds`, every worker in place of those there were, `[]` for nobody, up to `MAX_WORKERS`, instead of `assigneeId`; the listing is narrowed by `worker=` instead of `assignee=`, and `unassigned=true` keeps the tasks with no worker; the refusal is `task.invalid_worker`; `task.updated` names the field `workers`; a run taking a task puts its person among the workers, beside those there were. `TaskFilter.assignee` is `worker`. The migration moves each task's assignee into `task_workers` and keeps the column, unused, until the next release.
  
  A listing is searched by the words of a title: `GET .../tasks?q=`, every word in the title whatever the case, a number with or without `#` finding the task of it; and `GET .../goals?q=`, the roadmap being paged now (`limit`, `offset`, `more`) and narrowed by `open=`. `TaskFilter.q`; `GoalFilter` with `q`, `open` and a page. The command: `console tasks --search <words>`, `console goals --search <words> [--limit <n>] [--offset <n>]`, and `console task new|edit --worker <login|me|none>`, repeated or with commas between, in place of `--assignee`.
  
  The pages: `TaskForm` takes `people` as the workers to choose from, and `findTasks` and `findGoals`, searches a page at a time by which the task it is part of and the goal it works towards are found, in place of `parents` and `goals` whole; `Picker` is the component that searches, `PICKER_PAGE_LIMIT` its page; `TaskView` takes `parent`, `findTasks` and `findGoals`; `Workers` shows the people at work on a task as pictures side by side; `workloadOf` counts a task for each of its workers; `useConsoleData` offers `findTasks` and `findGoals` among its actions and reads the goals page after page. The Korean pages say 작업자 for what was 배정.

- [`7684597`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/76845971fb049842e774d6aed4811d817645c1a5) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A workspace is named by convention: the organization or the person the console is for, then Console, as SkillCDN Console is named. The skill deploying-a-console (1.4) suggests the name from the request or the organization's website and has the person confirm it, never inventing one; building-a-console (1.5) and the deploy README say the same of `WORKSPACE_NAME`. When the console connects a pack at SkillCDN it calls itself by the workspace's name with Console after it once, never `Acme Console console`.

- [`bb83073`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/bb8307353e1b1d7683eb34591cc8b723605ac245) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - Before 1.0 a command and a console of one SkillCDN line go together whatever the patch (ADR-0038): `commonRange` is the line, and each side names the oldest version of the other it works with, `CLIENT_POLICY.atLeast` for the console and the new `CONSOLE_AT_LEAST` for the command, raised only by a release that changes what passes between them, with a `Breaking:` changeset; both are 0.3.2, the release that brought the workers. `judgeConsole` and `consoleRange` stand beside `judgeClient` and `clientRange`. The command refuses a console older than it works with itself, as `console.outdated` in the console's form, with exit code `4` and the command that installs the console's version; `runCli` takes `consoleAtLeast` in its io for a console built on the package; `whoami` says the floor. `pnpm check:version` holds the floors to the line and to the version the pending changesets make.

- [`bb83073`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/bb8307353e1b1d7683eb34591cc8b723605ac245) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - Breaking: `console tasks --due week` keeps the tasks due by the last day of this week, the week beginning as the workspace says or, left to the language, as the person's language has it, else the machine's, in place of seven days from today (ADR-0039); the command asks `GET /api/v1/me` for it on that flag. Who has what counts what is due this week in place of seven days: `WorkloadRow.dueSoon` is `dueThisWeek`, `workloadOf` takes the day the week begins on, and `Workload` takes `weekStart`; the words `calendar.workload.dueSoon` are `dueThisWeek`. My work's bucket for this week ends with the week the same way. `CliIo` takes `locale`, the machine's language and region, for what follows a language where the person chose none.

## 0.3.1

### Patch Changes

- [`7a95f75`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/7a95f7577b2a49184b58c26a440019508f965798) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The package is built on SkillCDN 0.3.1: `@skillcdn/core`, `@skillcdn/client`, `@skillcdn/indexer`, `@skillcdn/reader` and `@skillcdn/brand` are taken at 0.3.1, which brings the contract of a command connecting to a deployment with a code a person approves (its ADR-0059: the routes under `/api/v1/connect`, their schemas, `parseConnectCode` and `connectPagePath` in `@skillcdn/core`; `connect`, `claimConnection`, `connection` and `decideConnection` in `@skillcdn/client`); nothing of the console reads it yet.

- [`420c882`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/420c88207cc6c3e04855deb2874e9ae444c87939) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - `console use` prints, after binding a directory to a project, the sentence for the checkout's agreement (AGENTS.md, or CLAUDE.md where that is the file agents read), to be copied as it is, so that every agent working there is sent to the console: nothing tells an agent to look until the checkout says so, as `skillcdn use` now prints the sentence for a pack. `THE_SENTENCE` is exported from the command's directory module, and the help and the spec say so.

- [`84ede12`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/84ede12e2d94871003f1c1f6a90f73f0e7a2ae0b) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - Every command that makes something prints its id on a line of its own, named by kind, so that an agent reads it rather than assumes it: `console task new` prints `task id:`, `console goal new` `goal id:`, `console ask` `decision id:`, and `console take` a line with `run id:` and `task id:` beside the task's number. The skill working-the-board says that a number or an id is read from the output of the command that made it, or from `--json`, and never assumed, since several agents write on one board.

- [`eb776aa`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/eb776aaab3d16cc99eaa574a5b143cf7d06a372b) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A private pack is connected with a code a person approves at SkillCDN (ADR-0036), in place of a token pasted: `POST /api/v1/projects/<key>/pack-token/connect` asks the deployment to connect the project's address as the `skillcdn` command does and answers the code and the page the person is shown (`restPackConnectStartedSchema`); `GET .../pack-token/connect` says how it stands, `none`, `pending`, `connected` with the project once the token is kept, or `ended` with why (`restPackConnectSchema`, `PACK_CONNECT_ENDINGS`); `DELETE .../pack-token/connect` gives it up. The client gains `connectPackToken`, `packTokenConnection` and `cancelPackTokenConnection` on a project; the project's pack token page says what the token is for and offers the two ways side by side, `PackConnect`, which asks the console again at the interval the deployment named, and the paste (`PackTokenForm` takes `setAt`, and offers to replace a token held); `console project pack-token <key> --connect` prints the page and the code and waits for the approval. The refusals are `project.pack_connect_unsupported` and `project.pack_connect_refused`, beside `skills.unavailable`. The paste stays for a token made by hand.

- [`bc86c15`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/bc86c1547e792d48ea93a72dab30584d0badb23b) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The skills the package carries take an organization's look from its website by default: deploying-a-console (1.3) asks for the website and, given one, plans the look and builds the organization's own console into the image as `console-ui/`; building-a-console (1.4) reads the marks and the colours from the site as its new reference, the-look-from-a-website.md, says, and maps them onto the pages with the contrast checked and the type taken only as the bundling rules allow. Nothing of the code changed.

- [`9793ad5`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/9793ad50b2cafff61321037c505d87c1193c3cc7) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The workspace holds a SkillCDN token of its own (ADR-0036): every project that names no address of its own and holds no token of its own reads the organization's pack with it. `PUT` and `DELETE /api/v1/workspace/pack-token` set it and take it away, and `POST`, `GET` and `DELETE /api/v1/workspace/pack-token/connect` connect it with a code a person approves, all for an administrator (`REST_ROUTES.workspacePackToken`, `restWorkspacePackConnectSchema`; the refusals `workspace.pack_token_refused`, `workspace.pack_token_unsupported`, `workspace.pack_connect_refused` and `workspace.pack_connect_unsupported`); `me.workspace.skills.packToken` says when it was set (`restWorkspaceSchema`, optional for an older console). The client gains `setWorkspacePackToken`, `removeWorkspacePackToken`, `connectWorkspacePackToken`, `workspacePackTokenConnection` and `cancelWorkspacePackTokenConnection`. The Settings page's Skills section shows the token, with a link that says to set one until one is held and to change it after, as a project's settings do, and an administrator sets it at `/settings?pack-token` on `PackTokenWays`, the two ways to a token side by side, which the project's page shares (`PackConnect` takes a `PackConnectState` and calls `onConnected` with nothing; `PackTokenForm` takes `setAt`). The command gains `console workspace pack-token --connect|--stdin|--remove`. Wherever people read it, the pack token is called the SkillCDN token; the API and the command keep `pack-token`. The setting of who sees whom is called the member visibility on the pages, and its values read as a scope.

- [`cfd6533`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/cfd6533ab41d55b23d47402336b3b5dcad264d14) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The workspace keeps the day its calendars begin a week on and its time zone (ADR-0035): `weekStart` (`auto`, the system default, as the language and region of each person's device have it, or `monday`, `sunday`; `WEEK_STARTS`) and `timeZone` (an IANA name, or `null`) on `me.workspace` (`restWorkspaceSchema`, both optional for an older console) and in `PATCH /api/v1/workspace` (`restWorkspacePatchSchema`, a zone the console knows, `request.invalid` otherwise; `timeZoneName` is the shape). `firstDayOfWeek(language, weekStart)` takes the workspace's word, `deviceLanguage` is the language the system default follows, and `Calendar`, `Week` and `WorkList` take `weekStart`; `weekStartOf` and `timeZoneOf` read a workspace's. The Settings page's Workspace section and form show and change both, each setting a row with its words beside it, the zone picked by area with its offset (`timeZoneGroups`, `deviceTimeZone`, `offsetOf`), the device's offered. The command prints moments in the workspace's time zone, as ISO 8601 with its offset, as its login kept it at `login`, `whoami` or `workspace` (the credentials file gains `timeZone` on a login), and as the API gives them, in UTC, while none is known or with `--json`; `moment` and `setMomentZone` are the command's; `console workspace edit` takes `--week-start` and `--time-zone`.

- [`7b70813`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/7b70813d3e9f2dae29ab228b7c4dc474cb96d238) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - Breaking: who may see whom is decided by one policy of the console, and the workspace keeps the settings it depends on (ADR-0035). `GET /api/v1/projects/<key>/members` answers, for a project open to the workspace, everyone active of the workspace, each with their role and `listed`, whether a listing holds them (`restMemberSchema`, optional, so that an older console still answers); `POST .../members` with a role of `member` is refused there as `member.open_project`, since everyone is a member already, and `PATCH .../members/<id>` makes an owner of anyone of the workspace, or takes an owner back to the member everyone is. `GET /api/v1/people` answers the people the asker may see, everyone or, when the workspace says `shared_projects`, the active people a member shares a project with, themselves and the administrators, the logins let in and the organization's reading going to whom it shows everyone; it takes `?login=` to find one person by their exact login, anyone active for an owner of a project, which `client.findPerson` reads and the command's `member`, `person` and `--assignee` use. `GET /api/v1/events` of the workspace is an administrator's while the people are narrowed. `PATCH /api/v1/workspace` changes the settings for an administrator (`restWorkspacePatchSchema`), the board hears `workspace.updated` (a new `EVENT_KINDS` entry) and `GET /api/v1/me` carries them in `workspace` (`restWorkspaceSchema`, `peopleVisibility` optional); `PEOPLE_VISIBILITIES` is the vocabulary. The pages: the Settings page has a Workspace section with the form at `/settings?workspace` (`WorkspaceSettingsView`, `WorkspaceSettingsForm`, `peopleVisibilityOf`), the Members page of an open project says so and shows everyone with an owner made from a row and nothing added or removed (`MemberList` takes `open` and `lookup`, a login input for an owner whose People page does not show everyone), the project form and settings say `Open: everyone of the workspace` or `Private: only those listed`, and the People page says what it shows. The command gains `console workspace` and `console workspace edit --people-visibility <everyone|shared-projects>`, and says how an owner is listed in an open project.

## 0.3.0

### Minor Changes

- [`e3e7c13`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/e3e7c1375d0d4d3c7399cd62ef9a98e84e8d8a3a) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - Breaking: the pack of a project is read as the `skillcdn` command reads one (ADR-0031), and the read routes of the console's API answer what the pack answered, in the shapes of `@skillcdn/core`: `GET .../skills/one`, `.../skills/browse`, `.../skills/find` and `.../skills/file` carry it as `read` (`RestSkill`, `RestBrowse`, `RestFind`, `RestFile`), `null` unless `ready`, with the pack's own refusal beside the status as `refusal` (`restPackRefusalSchema`); `GET .../skills` keeps its `items` for the pages and carries the `mounts` answer as `mount` (`RestMount`); `source`, `page` and a skill's `page` are `null` for a pack read from a directory on the console's machine (`/local/<owner>/<name>`, `SKILLS_DIR`). `restSkillPageSchema`, `restSkillTextSchema`, `restSkillBrowseEntrySchema` and `restSkillFindItemSchema` are gone with the shapes of the console's own. The package is built on SkillCDN 0.3, `@skillcdn/core` 0.3.0.
  
  The command renders the pack as an MCP client's model is given it, with the renderers of `@skillcdn/core` and the reads named as the console's commands: `console skills` prints what a client is told on connect and every skill and document, `console skills <folder>` the folder whole, `console skills --search <words>` one page with `--cursor <cursor>` to continue, `console skill <path>` the skill whole with its rules first, `console skill --file <path>` the file whole; `--json` prints what the pack answered, the pages joined. The Skills page says when a pack is read from the console's machine.

- [`389d56b`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/389d56b9312ea4fa935fd5add203456f8f2d2efd) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The package is versioned on SkillCDN's line from here on (ADR-0034): the major and the minor are those of the one version of the main repository's packages the console is built on, `0.3` for this release, and the patch is the console's own, so `@skillcdn/console` 0.3.x is the console for SkillCDN 0.3. Within a line every release is a patch, whose entry starts with `Breaking:` where it changes or removes something that exists; the next line of SkillCDN is a minor. The packages the console is built on, `@skillcdn/core`, `@skillcdn/indexer`, `@skillcdn/reader`, `@skillcdn/client` and `@skillcdn/brand`, are taken at one version, SkillCDN 0.3.0 here.

### Patch Changes

- [`66f1ecb`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/66f1ecbd571d4791364c16dfcb1ed35df247810c) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A decision is answered in the person's own words where none of the options fits: the answer form has a line of one's own under the options, `POST .../decisions/<id>/answer` takes `own` in place of `option` (one of the two, `restAnswerInputSchema`), and the answer becomes one more option of the decision, marked `own: true` (`restDecisionOptionSchema`) and the one chosen, so that the command, the pages and the events read a label as before; `console decision` says when an answer is in the person's own words.
  
  The record of the decisions (`DecisionLog`) is rebuilt: a row shows the question with its task, who asked and when, through which agent, and the answer with the word given, marked when it is the person's own, with who answered and when; it turns by pages of twenty (`Pager`, `pageOf`, `RECORD_PAGE_SIZE`), each at an address (`?page=`, `decisionsHref`, and `page` on the `decisions` route, passed to `DecisionList` as `page` and `pageHref`), and the page reads the decisions whole, so that a record past one page of the API loses nothing. Its classes are `sc-decision-record`, `sc-decision-record-row`, `-asked`, `-answer`, `-question`, `-chosen`, `-note` and `-meta`, and `sc-pager` with `sc-pager-range`; `sc-decision-log` and `sc-decision-row*` stay the plate's, and `sc-decision-row-answer` is gone; `sc-option-own` is the line of one's own on the answer form. `Time` takes `precision="day"` for the day alone, with the moment as its title.

## 0.1.24

### Patch Changes

- [`0cd727c`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/0cd727c226e672984d88d1f23f0f7caa0cb0b603) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The week at the top of My work, and of another person's work page, shows the person's tasks that were done or dropped on their days, struck through as a project's calendar shows them, beside the open ones; the lists below keep to the open tasks. `GET /api/v1/work` and `GET /api/v1/people/<id>/work` carry them as `closed`, which `restWorkSchema` and `restPersonWorkSchema` take as optional, so that a console older than this one still answers; `WorkList` takes them in `work.closed`.

- [`184e1b1`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/184e1b1f73fe478e46369d84557cd1aa3a705383) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - What an agent meeting the command for the first time ran into, fixed. `console take` names another run of yours open in the project, with the `--run <id>` to say on every command on a run from then on; `report`, `hand-in`, `ask`, `finish` and `fail` show `[--run <id>]` in their help and refuse as `run.none` and `run.ambiguous` when they cannot tell the run. `console skills` prints each skill's path, which `console skill` takes, beside the URI a host's own connection takes, and `console skill` takes that URI too. `console whoami` and `console help` print where the skill working-the-board is on this machine. The notice of a command newer than the console says that nothing is to be done until something is refused, and exit code `4` is in the help's list. `console help write` says the title is shown above the body. The console's own rules read the skill before anything is made rather than before the command that prints them, and say less of how the command works, which the help says. The skill working-the-board (1.17) moves setting up, the sentence a repository carries and the terms into its references, so that what every run reads is shorter.

## 0.1.23

### Patch Changes

- [`9ca166e`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/9ca166e7f25907f0ec4e0b24906ee1302bfb27f4) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The root of the pack is `.` where the command says how to browse it: `console skills .`, since a Windows shell rewrites a lone `/` into a path before the command sees it (`/` is still taken where it arrives whole). A path the SkillCDN deployment refuses as not one is answered as nothing there (`not_found`), no longer as a deployment out of reach.

## 0.1.22

### Patch Changes

- [`e91ce53`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/e91ce53ebd7c41219aa96fe0ed97c86df7b1b9d8) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The command's help in two layers. `console help` is the map: every command in a line with what it does in a sentence, a many-formed command (`task`, `goal`, `project`) listing its forms without their flags, then what holds everywhere; it is about half as long as it was. `console help <command>` is the reference: the command in full, every form and flag, the limits and the refusals, as before. What the skill working-the-board and the rules of the board say of how the work goes is no longer repeated in the help. `CommandHelp` carries a `synopsis` for the overview and, for a many-formed command, an `overview` usage beside the full one.

- [`a1d32df`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/a1d32dfaf5d4a59391664473de0f20d4a0864056) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The pack browsed and searched through the console, as the deployment's own tools browse and search it. `console skills <folder>` shows what a folder of the pack holds (`/` for the root): its skills, its folders with how many skills and documents each holds and the README that introduces it, its files, page after page. `console skills --search <words> [<folder>] [--limit <n>]` finds the skills and the documents the words match, in relevance order, a skill with the files of it that matched. `console skills` names the pack and counts its skills and documents. `console skill --file <path>` reads a file of the pack without a skill's path beside it; the two together are refused as not understood. On the API, `GET .../skills/browse` and `GET .../skills/find`, with `skillBrowse` and `skillFind` on a project's client and the schemas `restSkillBrowseSchema` and `restSkillFindSchema`; the listing carries `pack` and `counts` when it is ready. The console's own rules name the new commands.

## 0.1.21

### Patch Changes

- [`f18177d`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/f18177dc4d138b698b0b0ae354831798114989e8) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The board in hand, second pass. The whole card follows the pointer while it is dragged, as wide as where it stands and held where it was taken, a faded one keeping its place; the title is part of the hold, so that the card is dragged from anywhere but its menu, and a click on the title opens the task unless the card was dragged. The cursor is `grab` and `grabbing` again. The card's menu is smaller and closes from its own button. A task is put away from any state (ADR-0030): the task's page offers it for every task not put away, the card's menu for every card given `onArchive`, and `console task archive` refuses no open task any more (`task.open` is gone from the refusals, and from the console's REST API, which a console of this version expects). The board's page shows the project's description beside its name on a wide window (`.sc-page-heading`, `.sc-page-lead`).

- [`4f62405`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/4f6240525b91d65f2be2c13d58d2bceb4aaabd1c) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The card in hand keeps its shape. The card that follows the pointer while it is dragged carries the menu button of the one in its place, inert, so that the title keeps the room the button takes and wraps as it did; the dots are shown on it as on a hovered card.

- [`6b0575e`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/6b0575e8872840ce3aea48f9cb52760a2a2f6425) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A column's frame stands in front of what scrolls within it: the board's lit edge is drawn over the cards, so that a card scrolled to the edge of its column goes under the frame instead of covering it.

- [`951fe48`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/951fe4877b55fb9ccc7fdbe87c798bf9602f616c) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The way from the pack to the board. The console's own rules (`REFERENCE_RULES`) gain a third item under "Start here, every time": work given in chat with no task on the board yet is written on the board first (`console task new`), its number told to the person, taken and worked as any task, without asking whether to; until then the board is only read. The first item says the skill working-the-board is read before the first command of a run, with the command that prints where it is. `console help` says the same in its preamble, and `console whoami`, in a directory that works in a project, adds a line pointing to `console rules` and to the skill, so that an agent brought to the console by a pack's rules finds the next step there.

## 0.1.20

### Patch Changes

- [`a304e8a`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/a304e8ad641719619bcfab9165fe179326926396) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The board in hand. A card is dragged to another column from anywhere on it but its title and its menu: with a mouse at once, under a finger once it has rested a moment, so that a finger that moves scrolls the page as before; the cursor is the standard `move`, which every platform draws itself. The state select is gone from the card; the task's page keeps the way for the keyboard. Each card has a menu in its corner, three dots, with the way to the task's form (`Board` and `TaskCard` take `editHref` and `onEdit`) and, once the task is done or dropped, the way to put it away (`onArchive`). `Board` no longer takes `archiveHref` and no column leads to the archive; the default console leads there from the board's page, beside the way to write a task, and the archive page says nothing above its list. `TaskCard` takes `onPress` in place of `onGrip`, and the words `board.grip`, `board.moveTo` and `archive.lead` are gone from the packs, `board.menu` added.

- [`dfb88e5`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/dfb88e5629df127dbfcfec65567c24cb39cbe00c) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The pictures wherever they were put. A picture handed in on a run is shown on the task's page as a comment's is: a thumbnail under what the agent handed in, large over the page when pressed, and placed where a report or the summary names it as `![words](its name)` (`RunCard` resolves the names against the run's files). A picture attached to a page of the documents is shown where the body names it, as the text has it (`DocumentView` resolves the names against the page's files), and `DocumentForm` takes a picture pasted or chosen into the body, placing `![](its name)` in the text and handing the files up with `onSubmit` (`files`), which the default console attaches once the page is written. `isPicture` is exported from the package as before, now from the components' `files` module with `placed`, `placing`, `namedFile` and `unusedName` beside it; the words `docs.form.pictures`, `addPictures`, `pictureHint`, `removeFile` and `runs.picture` are added to the packs.

## 0.1.19

### Patch Changes

- [`b1d2ecb`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/b1d2ecbe2a9b006b6ade43c323c362014f750ed2) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The command keeps a login per console and account, and a token goes only to the console that issued it (ADR-0027). `console login` at a second console adds a login beside the first, which is the default console; `--default` marks another. `console logins` lists the consoles signed in to, never a token. `console logout` takes `--url` and `--account`. `console use` takes `--url` and `--account` and writes them into `.skillcdn-console.json` where the directory is bound to a console other than the default or to one account of several; the file never carries a secret and drops keys it does not know. Which console a command talks to is resolved first (`--url`, `CONSOLE_URL`, the directory's file, the default login) and the token is the one kept for that console alone: `--url` for a console nobody is signed in to here is refused instead of sending another console's token, `CONSOLE_URL` and `CONSOLE_TOKEN` are taken together or not at all, an address is https or this machine, and a redirect is never followed. The credentials file of before is read as one login and rewritten in the new shape; one the command cannot read is never written over.

- [`553117c`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/553117c07ed602779cfa8548dbf7be1da187c58d) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The console's own rules no longer make a push a decision on the board: whether and how a change is committed and pushed is the repository's own agreement and the task's word, and the board is asked only what the task leaves open, what costs money and what deletes. The skills' shared rules say the same of a push.

- [`36df526`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/36df526b48b108e7b12a1971e01515efd5940abf) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A picture in a comment is a thumbnail that opens large over the page: zoom with the wheel, the buttons or two fingers, move with a drag, close with Escape, the close button or a click beside the picture, and the focus returns to the thumbnail. `PictureThumb`, `PictureOverlay`, `Lightbox` and `usePicture` are exported for a console of your own, and `Markdown` takes `onOpenPicture`.

- [`7ea0639`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/7ea06398efbfdd0f1126a81135a427506a515547) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A task done or dropped is put away (ADR-0029): out of the board, the lists and the calendar, counted by its goal as before, open by its number still, and brought back at any time. From the task's page or `console task archive <number>`; every closed task of a goal at once with `console task archive --goal <id>` or from the goal's page, offered when the goal is closed; and by the console itself after `TASK_ARCHIVE_AFTER_DAYS`. The archive is a page of the project (`/archive`), read a page at a time; `console tasks --archived` lists it; `console task restore <number>` brings a task back. A task carries `archivedAt`, the events `task.archived` and `task.restored` are new, and the pages now read a project's current tasks whole, page after page, instead of the first five hundred.

- [`3119131`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/31191313e22430fd647973115824f1bb8c3de401) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - `console tasks --unassigned` lists the tasks assigned to nobody (`unassigned=true` on the REST listing, and `unassigned` on the client's task filter); `console member add <login> --project a,b,c` lists a person in several projects at once, one line per project, one listed already said so; and the console's own rules name what the person whose token the command holds says as an instruction, next to the task, the rules and the answers on the board.

- [`c1a68ae`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/c1a68ae4e45533c37d3498d940da87e3f2ee4ed4) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A goal's page reads as the board does: its facts in a row at the head, and its tasks under a switch between the board of them, the list by state and the calendar (`?view=`, `?month=`), the board opening by default. A card is dragged by its grip to another column to move the task, on the project's board and a goal's alike, with a mouse or a finger; the select stays for the keyboard. `GoalView` takes `view`, `viewHref`, `board` and `calendar`; `goalHref` takes options in place of the form; `goalMonth`, `columnStateOf`, `GOAL_TASK_VIEWS` and `GoalTaskView` are exported.

- [`4848351`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/4848351e6a00daa9cae976d1df3e6461251484be) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - An agent reads the project's pack through the console (ADR-0028): `console skill <path>` reads one skill whole, its rules first, as `load_skill` lays it out, and `console skill <path> --file <path>` a file of the pack; the API serves them at `GET .../skills/one` and `GET .../skills/file`. A private pack is read with a pack token an owner sets, on the Settings page or with `console project pack-token <key> --stdin`, which the console keeps sealed and shows to nobody; the project says when it was set (`packToken`), and the status `token_invalid` says when the deployment refuses it. The client gains `skill`, `skillFile`, `setPackToken` and `removePackToken`; `PackTokenForm` is exported.

- [`dd0f6ff`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/dd0f6ff64e98f6822552844df5262ba2f9cea338) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The skill `working-the-board` carries the one sentence a repository's agreement copies to bind its checkouts to a console, and this repository's own agreement carries it first.

## 0.1.18

### Patch Changes

- [`ab65be0`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/ab65be0818f3d4fb0794bba786db9e6be7362898) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A goal is planned in days and sorted into a category, and the roadmap is read as months (ADR-0026). A goal carries `startsOn` and `category` beside its `dueOn`; `POST` and `PATCH .../goals` take them (`goal.invalid_dates`, `goal.invalid_category`), `GET .../goals?category=&state=` narrows the roadmap, and a category goals are sorted into cannot be removed (`category.in_use`). The client takes a `GoalFilter` on `goals()`. `GoalTimeline` shows the months as columns and each goal as a bar from its start to its target in the colour of its category, filled as far as its tasks are done, with today marked, and `timelineBars` lays it out; `Calendar` takes `goalHref` and marks each goal's target day on the month; `GoalForm` takes `categories` and offers the start day; `GoalList` and `GoalView` show the category and the days; `roadmapHref(key, from)` names the roadmap from a month on. The command gets `goal new --starts --category`, `goal edit --starts <day|none> --category <key|none>`, and `goals --category --state`.

- [`92ab63e`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/92ab63ead79e659c496a89c7298885b75a4340dd) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The command names its version on every request, and the console holds it to the versions it takes (ADR-0025). `console` sends `user-agent: skillcdn-console/<version> (cli; ...)`; every answer of the API carries `console-version` and `console-client-range`, and `GET /api/v1/me` answers them as `console`. Before 1.0 a console takes a command from its own version on and within its minor; from 1.0 the same major and a minor within one, either way. A command outside the range is refused with `426` and `client.outdated` or `client.too_new`; it exits with `4` and prints the update command for where it is installed (for the machine, in a project, or run again from a cache, with the package manager that put it there). Within the range, a command older or newer than the console is told once a day on stderr; `console whoami` says the versions and where the command is installed every time. `createClient` takes `userAgent`; `@skillcdn/console/api` exports the `compat` module (`parseVersion`, `judgeClient`, `clientRange`, `userAgentOf`, `clientVersionOf`, the headers, and `CLIENT_POLICY`, which narrows the common rule and never widens it); a token carries `client`, the version of the command that last presented it, which `TokenList` shows with whether the console still takes it (`consoleVersion`). The package now exports its `package.json`.

## 0.1.17

### Patch Changes

- [`503b545`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/503b54523d15c5acb84b76fe5575ebc60f3d3779) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A task carries a discussion (ADR-0024). People and agents comment on a task in Markdown, with pictures and files put in: `console comment <number> "<markdown>" [--attach <file>]...`, `--file <path>` or `-`, which needs no run; `console file <id> [--out <path>]` saves any file of the project, a run's, a comment's or a page's, on the agent's machine, so that a picture a person put on the board is looked at. `console task` and `console take` print the discussion with the task, each file with the command that reads it. The client has `comments`, `comment` and `readFile` on a project, and `fileUrl` names any file of the project by its id; the schemas have `restCommentSchema`, `restCommentsSchema` and `restCommentInputSchema`, the limits `MAX_COMMENTS_PER_TASK` and `MAX_FILES_PER_COMMENT`, the event kind `task.commented`. The task's page reads the comments, the runs and the decisions as one thread in the order they came, with the form at its end, where a picture pasted in goes with the comment and is placed in the text: `TaskThread`, `CommentCard`, `CommentForm`, `threadOf` and `isPicture` are exported; `TaskView` takes `comments` and `onComment`, and its `fileHref` now takes a file's id; `Markdown` takes `fileSrc` for the pictures a text names. The console's rules and the skill `working-the-board` say when a comment is the word, and that work given in chat is written on the board first.

- [`ca71b3f`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/ca71b3fe28faee4edabbcb9d78ef384cac728860) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A project's mark shows its symbol only until its image is shown, and again should the image fail: an image with clear parts, a logo on a transparent background, no longer shows the symbol through it.

- [`64bba0d`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/64bba0d7cc7c4b4c5087983d9e9bf6fb5d6cb444) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The tab bar no longer shows a vertical scrollbar on a desktop: the current tab's underline reached one pixel past the bar, which scrolls sideways, so browsers that show scrollbars offered to scroll it down. The bar itself now lies over the header's line, and nothing in it scrolls down.

## 0.1.16

### Patch Changes

- [`cebb1a0`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/cebb1a05782c5511fbe36823f6d39814fa7c9f4c) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The console's rules say what a task's title is: what is to be done, in plain words that stand on their own, with what it refers to in the body. The task form says so under its title, and `console task new` says so in its limits.

- [`3807f32`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/3807f328de04091edd7381ec41a607e474c1aa70) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The Decisions page says "No decision waits for a person" where there is none, in both languages.

- [`e73a807`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/e73a80778a36aeff4d4d8a0be5c46bc76d9a9ed3) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - `console take` says that a run was taken over only when one was open on the task, and prints the task as it stands once taken.

- [`957c774`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/957c774e800826dbce8f7080e757716c613af1f5) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A project's mark shows its symbol until its own image is there, so that it is never empty while the image loads.

- [`e41a3b7`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/e41a3b7f94a34d9c4a1781977d6097cacc2c2c32) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The calendar shows the task under the pointer in full: a card with its number and title and the facts its page lists (state, priority, category, goal, owner, assignee, plan, and when it was started, done, written and changed), above or below the item where there is more room, kept within the window (`TaskTip`, placed by `placeTip`; `Calendar` takes the project's `goals` to name a task's goal). Phones keep the lists below the calendar instead, as before. The spinner turns whatever the motion preference, slowly where motion is to be reduced. The sign-in page's line now reads "Join an organization made of AI agents."

## 0.1.15

### Patch Changes

- [`2cab124`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/2cab12443cd813f5c4d35c4680b9ab1a311b1969) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A person's work, seen by whoever oversees them.
  
  - **The REST API.** `GET /api/v1/people/<id>/work` answers another person's plate, for an administrator or for an owner of a project the person is in (everyone of the workspace is in a project open to it; a private one holds those listed), signed in or through their agent: the person's open tasks within the projects the asker may see and no other, each with its project, the sooner due first; no decisions, which wait for anyone. `auth.forbidden` for anyone else, `person.not_found` for an id that is nobody's.
  - **The command.** `console work --person <login>` shows it, as `console work` shows one's own.
  - **The client.** `client.personWork(personId)`, `restPersonWorkSchema` and `RestPersonWork`, `personWorkPath(personId)`.
  - **The default UI.** `/people/<id>/work` (`personWorkHref(id, week?)`, the route `person-work`, with `?week=` as My work has it): the week and the lists of My work under the person's name, without the decisions; a "Work" link beside each person on the People page for an administrator or an owner, and beside each member on a project's Members page for its owners; a plate that is not the asker's to see says so. `WorkList` takes `work.decisions` as optional and leaves the section out when it is left out; `PeopleList` and `MemberList` take `workHref`. Words under `people`: `work`, `workOf`, `loadingWork`, `couldNotLoadWork`, `noWork`, `notOverseen`.

- [`603d417`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/603d417f38f7daf4c35a1480ef83ae1694912c62) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The calendar says whose day it is, and the Agents page what to paste.
  
  - **A task with one day alone**, a due day or a start, sits on that day as any task on its day: a dot in its category's colour before its title, and the title in the tip; a task with a start alone is never late. The mark for a start alone (`sc-calendar-task-start`) and the word `calendar.startsOnly` are gone.
  - **Who has a task**, on a project's calendar: the person's picture, or their initial, stands before the title, on a bar and on a day alike, and the tip names them; nothing for a task nobody has, and nothing on the week of My work or of a person's work, which is one person's. `Avatar` takes the size `xs`.
  - **The words for an agent**, on the Agents page, are a text to paste as it is, in a box with a button that copies it: the two commands, `npm install -g @skillcdn/console` and `console login --url <origin>`, and what to tell back. The paragraph on approving is gone, since the agent says what to do next; `connect.words.thenApprove` with it, and `connect.words.quote` now answers several lines. `CopyButton` is exported, with the words `common.copy` and `common.copied`; `agents.fresh.copy` and `agents.fresh.copied` are gone, and `NewToken` copies through it. New class: `sc-copyable`.
  - **The People page says add**: "Add a person", "Add", "Added, not signed in yet", "Added by an administrator", and the feed's "added <login>", in both languages; the keys stay (`people.letIn`, `people.letInAria`, `people.notSignedIn`, `people.source.listed`, `people.form.submit`, `feed.event.personListed`).

- [`ee265e4`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/ee265e48cd2edfe5db710a9942605300d6474e15) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The calendars tell a cut title at once. On a project's calendar and on the week of My work, a task whose title its line cut short, or a bar on a day where it shows no title, now shows the whole title in a tip of the console's own the moment the pointer is on it, or the item is focused, in place of the browser's tooltip that came after a wait. The items carry `data-tip` and the class `sc-tip`, and `data-clipped` while the tip applies; a bar no longer clips its own box, its title does.

- [`4e331c8`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/4e331c8762f74f8ffa6504ed4a1bf59edd4cb127) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The organization at the git host says who is in (ADR-0022).
  
  - **The REST API.** `GET /api/v1/people` answers `organization` beside `items` and `listed`: the organization at the git host whose members are in, where the deployment names one (`host`, `name`, `syncedAt`, when the worker last read it whole, or `null` until it has), or `null`. A person the organization let in carries `source: "organization"`, and `PATCH /api/v1/people/<id>` refuses to change their standing (`person.of_organization`).
  - **The client.** `restOrganizationSchema` and `RestOrganization`.
  - **The default UI.** The People page says which organization decides who is in and when it was last read; `PeopleList` takes `organization`; `useConsoleData` answers `organization`. Words under `people`: `organization`, `organizationRead`, `organizationNotRead`.
  - **The deployment.** `GITHUB_APP_ID`, `GITHUB_APP_PRIVATE_KEY` and `GITHUB_ORG` name the GitHub App installed on the organization and the organization: asked at sign-in, and read whole by the worker every ten minutes, so that those who left are out and those who returned are in, by the account's id.

- [`51a0a89`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/51a0a89d8aa8f2ffa6f2c2b0975929a40b7e25df) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The rules of the board: what an organization asks of its agents, on the settings (ADR-0023).
  
  - **Rules** are pages the workspace and each project keep at `rules`. The console's own hold until an administrator writes the organization's, and change with the console's version; a project adds its own or none, written by an owner. `GET` and `PUT /api/v1/rules`, `POST /api/v1/rules/archive`, `GET /api/v1/rules/versions`; `GET /api/v1/projects/<key>/rules` answers both halves; a project's are written as the page `rules`, kept out of the folders and the search. The client gains `rules`, `writeRules`, `archiveRules` and `rulesVersions`, and a project's `rules`; the API layer `restRulesSchema`, `restProjectRulesSchema`, `restRulesInputSchema`, `RULES_SOURCES`, `REFERENCE_RULES`, `PROJECT_RULES_TEMPLATE`, `RULES_PATH`, `RULES_TITLE`, `isReferenceRules`, `MAX_RULES_LENGTH` and `rulesPath`.
  - **The command**: `console rules` prints what holds; `console rules write` writes a project's or, with `--workspace`, the workspace's, and `none` puts them away; `console take` prints the rules with the task; `console skills` says where a person connects an agent that has no connection to the address, and where the console's own skills are on the machine (`CliIo.skillsDir`).
  - **The pages**: a Settings tab on the workspace (`/settings`), with the rules, the categories and where the skills come from; the Categories tab moves under it, its address unchanged. A project's Settings are read by every member, with the project's form at `?edit` and its rules at `?rules`. `RulesView`, `RulesForm`, `settingsHref` and `projectSettingsHref` are exported; `ProjectForm` takes `skillsSource` and `organizationAddress`, and shows the deployment's origin before the address typed; the Skills page says how an agent is connected.
  - `GET /api/v1/me` carries `workspace.skills`: the SkillCDN deployment's origin and the organization's address, or `null`.
  - Words: `settings.*`, `rules.*`, `projects.settings.edit`, `projects.settings.organizationsSkills`, `projects.settings.noSkills`, `projects.form.skillsAddressHint`, `skills.connect` and `skills.connectLink`; `nav.categories` and `projects.settings.onlyOwner` are gone. New classes: `sc-rules`, `sc-rules-facts`, `sc-input-group`, `sc-input-prefix`, `sc-category-chips`.

- [`b03a2ff`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/b03a2ff5868bb595f4065f434744fc367ee72c67) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The rules say the person's language, a token gives itself up, and the settings pages are tidied.
  
  - **The rules** may say `{language}` for the language of the person an agent works for: `console rules` and `console take` fill it with the name of the language the person chose for the pages (`GET /api/v1/me`), or with the task's language when they chose none, and `RulesView` fills it with the language the pages speak; the API answers the text as written. The console's own rules say so where they speak of reports, and no longer repeat what every agent does with code and commits. `fillRules`, `languageNameOf` and `RULES_LANGUAGE_PLACEHOLDER` are exported.
  - **Back to the console's own:** the console's own rules saved as they stand while the organization's are written put the organization's page away rather than copy it (`PUT /api/v1/rules`); the settings offer "Reset to the console's rules" on the page and on the form (`RulesForm` takes `onReset`).
  - **`console logout`** gives the token up at the console (`DELETE /api/v1/me/token`, `client.giveUpToken()`) and forgets it; a console out of reach leaves the token to the Agents page, and the person is told so.
  - **An agent's name:** `console login` without `--agent` names the agent it runs under where its environment says so (Claude Code, Codex), else "An agent", with the machine after it ("Claude Code on a laptop"); the pages show the name before "on" and the whole on hover (`agentLabel`).
  - **The pages:** a project that is not private is marked "Open to everyone" wherever projects are listed; a project's settings keep its facts in one list with its description among them, and fold the workspace's rules under where they come from; the Skills page says when the console could not reach SkillCDN instead of loading on, and the console answers `unavailable` rather than failing when the deployment stops answering half-way. Words: `rules.read`, `projects.public`; classes `sc-facts-plain`, `sc-facts-wide`, `sc-section-head`, `sc-rules-actions`, `sc-rules-fold`.
  - The console's own rules say they come with the task: `console take` prints them under it, read once there, and `console rules` reads them any time; the skill `working-the-board` (1.5) and the command's overview say the same.

- [`e752f2b`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/e752f2ba711991eb011a46ce5e52d7995235dbbf) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - Who is in is the console's own record, and an administrator keeps it (ADR-0022).
  
  - **A person has a standing.** Every person carries `status` (`active` or `deactivated`), `source` (what let them in: `organization`, `domain` or `listed`) and `deactivatedAt`. A deactivated person is kept with everything they did, refused on every request with their agents, and offered to no picker. `PERSON_STATUSES`, `PersonStatus`, `MEMBERSHIP_SOURCES` and `MembershipSource` name the words; `vocabulary.status` says them.
  - **The REST API.** `GET /api/v1/people` answers `listed` beside `items`: the logins an administrator let in that nobody has signed in with yet. `PATCH /api/v1/people/<id>` takes `status` beside `role` (`person.of_organization` for a person the organization let in; `person.last_admin` keeps one administrator in). `POST /api/v1/listings` lets a login in (`listing.exists`, `listing.unknown_host`) and `DELETE /api/v1/listings/<id>` takes one away (`listing.not_found`). The workspace's feed gains `person.listed`, `person.unlisted`, `person.deactivated` and `person.reactivated`.
  - **The client.** `client.createListing(input)`, `client.removeListing(id)`, `restListingSchema`, `restListingInputSchema`, `RestListing`, `RestListingInput`, `restPath("listings", id)`, `REST_ROUTES.listings`, `hostOfLogin(login)` and `isLoginAt(host, login)`, `MAX_LOGIN_LENGTH`.
  - **The command.** `console people` lists the deactivated as such and the logins let in; `console person add <login> [--host gh|google]`, `person remove <login>`, `person deactivate <login>` and `person reactivate <login>`.
  - **The default UI.** The People page keeps those in, those let in and not signed in yet, and the deactivated apart, with the ways an administrator lets someone in (`/people/new`, the route `new-listing`, `PATHS.newListing`), takes a person out or back in, and takes a listing away. `PeopleList` takes `listed`, `onChangeStatus` and `onUnlist`; `ListingForm` is the form; `useConsoleData` answers `listed` and the actions `createListing` and `removeListing`. New classes: `sc-people-page`, `sc-people-section`, `sc-person-note`, `sc-person-row-out`, `sc-listing-form`, `sc-field-hint`. Words under `people`: `letIn`, `letInAria`, `notSignedIn`, `deactivated`, `deactivate`, `reactivate`, `leftOrganization`, `source`, `at`, `form`; under `feed.event`: `personListed`, `personUnlisted`, `personDeactivated`, `personReactivated`.
  - **The deployment.** `MEMBERS` is gone: who may sign in is the People page's, the Workspace domain's and, with the GitHub App, the organization's; `ADMINS` lets its logins in and makes them administrators at sign-in.

## 0.1.14

### Patch Changes

- [`b9d20df`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/b9d20dfc2e29f73cd52603243a42412a4ca68d5c) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - My work as a week, and the pages without the prose under their titles.
  
  - **The default UI.** My work opens on the week: the person's open tasks on its seven days, a task planned across days as a bar, each with its project's mark, today marked, and the weeks around a step away; the address says which week (`/work?week=<day>`). The lists below stay, a page at a time. The descriptions that stood under every page's title are gone; what remains under a title is what the page is about: a project's description on its board, its visibility on its members, the address the skills are served from.
  - **The package.** `Week` (`WeekProps`): one week of tasks on its own, with `weekHref` for the weeks around and `markOf` for what stands before a task; `WorkList` takes `week` and `weekHref`; `weekOf(day, firstDay)` and `formatDays(from, to, language)` beside the other days; `workHref(week?)`, and the route `work` carries `week`. The words of the descriptions left the packs (`projects.lead`, `projects.members.leadWorkspace`, `leadPrivate` and `leadRoles`, `projects.settings.leadBefore` and `leadAfter`, `decisions.recordLead`, `calendar.lead`, `calendar.workload.lead`, `roadmap.lead`, `work.lead`, `categories.lead`, `onlyAdmin` and `inUse`, `skills.howLoaded`, `people.lead`, `people.agentsLead`, `agents.lead`, `connect.lead`), `calendar.week` came in, and `skills.servedBy` is shorter. The styles gained `sc-week` and `sc-work-lists`.

## 0.1.13

### Patch Changes

- [`b4c2f7b`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/b4c2f7b54d8bc4f1d488ea3eb376b1c14f594f96) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The code `console login` shows is good for thirty minutes rather than ten, so that an agent waiting for a person who is away from their desk does not lose it; the pages say so.

- [`b4c2f7b`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/b4c2f7b54d8bc4f1d488ea3eb376b1c14f594f96) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - A token configures as its person does ([ADR-0021](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/blob/main/docs/adr/0021-a-token-configures-as-its-person-does.md)).
  
  - **The command.** Six commands for what an administrator or an owner does on the pages, so that an agent sets up what it is told to instead of handing its person a list of clicks: `people` and `person role <login> <admin|member>`; `project <key>`, `project new <key> "<name>"`, `project edit <key>` and `project icon <key> <file>|--remove`; `members`, `member add|role|remove <login>`; `category new|edit|remove <key>`. `console help` lists them under "Configuring, as the person", each with its limits and its refusals by code; the console decides with the same roles as on the pages, and refuses with `auth.forbidden` where the person may not. The tokens themselves have no command, by design.
  - **The client.** Every call a session may make, a token may make, within the person's roles: `createProject`, `updatePerson`, a project's `update`, `setIcon`, `addMember`, `updateMember` and `removeMember`, `createCategory`, `updateCategory`, `removeCategory`, and `updateMe` for the language; what they write names the agent beside the person. Only the tokens themselves, and the approval of a connection, stay a session's (`auth.session_required`).
  - **The default UI.** A console of a person's own, served on a token, offers the new project, the settings, the members, the categories and the people's roles as the organization's console does, and keeps the choice of language on the person rather than in the browser; only the Agents pages stay on the organization's console, signed in.

- [`b4c2f7b`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/b4c2f7b54d8bc4f1d488ea3eb376b1c14f594f96) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The README says that the command runs on Node.js 22 today, checked by hand on each release; the package keeps 24 as the floor it states and is tested on.

## 0.1.12

### Patch Changes

- [`2b7601c`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/2b7601c2c2722f56c1076b3c0f2db675049af5bf) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - People by name, pages of a console's own, and the words a person tells their agent.
  
  - **The default UI.** A person is shown by the name their host gives, with the login on hover, and by the login where there is no name: `PersonChip`, `Avatar`'s initial, the header's menu, the pickers of a person, the runs' labels and who-has-what; `personName` (from the components) is the rule. The Agents page tells a person to have their agent install `@skillcdn/console` from npm and run `console login --url <origin>`, in both languages.
  - **The composition.** `createConsole({ pages })` adds tabs of the console's own: a `CustomPage` has a `key`, a `label` (one word, or by language tag), a `level` (`workspace` or `project`), `wide`, and `render(context)`, given the data of `useConsoleData` with its actions, the project, the client, the navigation, the words and the language; the address is `/pages/<key>` or `/projects/<project>/pages/<key>` (`customPageHref`), and `customPageLabel` is the tab's word. The router knows the route `custom`. The README now lists the stylesheet's tokens by group, for a console that sets its own look.

## 0.1.11

### Patch Changes

- [`6b29782`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/6b297823b0ad0586b9151ec0a9c236e6cdb28a4e) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The second pass of milestone 9: a project's mark, the calendar's bars, the listings a page at a time, and no claim that an agent is at work ([ADR-0020](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/blob/main/docs/adr/0020-the-console-does-not-claim-an-agent-is-at-work-a-run-says-when-it-was-last-heard-from-and-a-new-run-takes-over.md)). Additive, apart from the listings' answers, which now carry `more`, a run's `lastHeardAt` and a project's `color` and `symbol`, which every answer now carries, the refusal `run.task_taken`, which is gone, and the `runs` prop of `Board`, which is gone with the badge it fed.
  
  - **The API client and the schemas.** A project carries `color` (one of `COLOR_NAMES`, or `null`) and `symbol` (up to `MAX_PROJECT_SYMBOL_LENGTH` characters with no whitespace, or `null`); `restProjectInputSchema` and `restProjectPatchSchema` take both, and a plate's `project` (`restWorkSchema`) carries them. `projectColorOf` and `projectSymbolOf` choose the mark the pages show where none is set: a colour from the key, never gray, and the first letter of the name. A project may carry an image of its own for the mark instead: `icon` (`{ sha256, contentType }`, `restProjectIconSchema`, or `null`), set with `client.project(key).setIcon(file)` and taken away with `removeIcon()`, read at `projectIconPath(key, icon)`; `PROJECT_ICON_TYPES` are the kinds allowed and `MAX_PROJECT_ICON_BYTES` the size. `useConsoleData`'s actions gain `setProjectIcon` and `removeProjectIcon`, and `createProject` takes the image as a second argument. `tasks()`, `decisions()` and `runs()` take `limit` and `offset` (`Page`) and answer `{ items, more }`, as `restTasksSchema`, `restDecisionsSchema` and `restRunsSchema` say; `DEFAULT_PAGE_LIMIT` is the command's page. A run carries `lastHeardAt`; `EVENT_KINDS` gains `run.taken_over`, since a run begun on a task takes over the one open on it. `COLOR_NAMES` and `ColorName` name the colours the pages paint; `CATEGORY_COLORS` is the same list.
  - **The command.** `tasks`, `decisions` and `runs` take `--limit <n>` and `--offset <n>`, show `DEFAULT_PAGE_LIMIT` lines at a time, and end with `... more: --offset <n>` when there is more; `runs` says when each open run was last heard from; `take` takes over a run open on the task, and `help take` says so; `projects` counts no agents at work.
  - **The default UI.** `ProjectMark` (a project's mark, `sm`, `md` or `lg`) on the projects page, in the header of a project's pages and on the rows of My work; `ProjectForm` takes a symbol and a colour, and an image, and shows the mark as it will be; `onSubmit` gets the image with the settings (a `File`, or `null` to take it away). `ProjectMark` shows the image when the project has one. `Calendar` draws a task planned across days as a bar from its start to its due day, in a lane of its own (`weekSegments`), with the colours of the `categories` given as its legend; the Calendar page is narrowed with `BoardFilters` as the board is, and `calendarHref(key, month, filter)` carries the filter with the month. `TaskList`, `EventFeed` and `DecisionLog` show `PAGE_SIZE` rows at a time with `ShowMore` (`usePaged` for a list of your own). `Board` takes no `runs` and a card names no agent; `RunCard` says when an open run was last heard from, and calls an open run open. The Categories page is in every member's navigation. The board no longer scrolls on its own below its columns. Both language packs changed accordingly.

## 0.1.10

### Patch Changes

- [`2f05962`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/2f05962efd56dd99a28e2f3efa02de92615d8680) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The schedule, the roadmap and the categories ([ADR-0019](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/blob/main/docs/adr/0019-a-task-is-planned-in-days-the-board-keeps-its-moments-and-goals-and-categories-give-the-work-its-shape.md)): a task is planned in days and the board keeps its moments, tasks are sorted into the workspace's categories, a project has a roadmap of goals whose progress is read from their tasks, and the pages that follow. Additive, apart from the fields every task and every event now carry and a board route that now carries its filter.
  
  - **The API client and the schemas.** A task carries `category` (its key, name and colour, or `null`), `goalId`, `startsOn`, `dueOn`, `startedAt` and `doneAt`; `restTaskInputSchema` and `restTaskPatchSchema` take `category` (a key), `goalId`, `startsOn` and `dueOn`. `tasks()` takes a `TaskFilter` (`state`, `open`, `category`, `goal`, `assignee`, `dueBy`). New on `client.project(key)`: `goals()`, `goal(id)`, `createGoal`, `updateGoal`; on the client: `categories()`, `createCategory`, `updateCategory`, `removeCategory`, `work()`. New schemas: `restCategorySchema`, `restCategoriesSchema`, `restCategoryInputSchema`, `restCategoryPatchSchema`, `restGoalSchema`, `restGoalsSchema`, `restGoalInputSchema`, `restGoalPatchSchema`, `restTaskCountsSchema`, `restWorkSchema`. The vocabulary gains `GOAL_STATES`, `CATEGORY_COLORS` and `OPEN_TASK_STATES`; `EVENT_KINDS` gains `category.*` and `goal.*`; an event carries `goalId`. New bounds: `MAX_CATEGORIES`, `MAX_CATEGORY_KEY_LENGTH`, `MAX_CATEGORY_NAME_LENGTH`. `REST_ROUTES` gains `categories` and `work`, `PROJECT_COLLECTIONS` gains `goals`, and `categoryPath(key)` is new.
  - **The command.** `console work` (what is on your plate across your projects), `console tasks --open --mine --category --goal --due <day|today|week|overdue>`, `console task new` with `--category --goal --starts --due --assignee --parent`, `console task edit`, `console goals`, `console goal <id>`, `goal new`, `goal edit`, and `console categories`. `console task` says the plan and the moments.
  - **The default UI.** The board's filters (`BoardFilters`, `filterTasks`, `byDue`; the address carries them, `boardHref`), the Calendar page (`Calendar`, `Workload`, `workloadOf`; `calendarHref`), the Roadmap page with a goal's own page and form (`GoalList`, `GoalView`, `GoalForm`; `goalHref`, `newGoalHref`), the workspace's My work page (`WorkList`) and Categories page (`CategoryList`), tasks as rows (`TaskList`), the decisions' record (`DecisionLog`), and the small blocks `CategoryBadge`, `DueBadge`, `GoalStateBadge`, `ProgressBar` and `Day`. `TaskForm` and `TaskView` take `categories` and `goals`; `Board` takes `runs` and names the agent at work on a card; `ConsoleComponents` names `Calendar`, `GoalList`, `WorkList` and `CategoryList`. The day helpers (`today`, `addDays`, `monthGrid`, `formatDay` and the rest) are exported. `useConsoleData` carries `categories` and `goals` with the actions on them. Both language packs grew.

## 0.1.9

### Patch Changes

- [`82d103a`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/82d103ad650a1b67c9809f7e72d019c0e6851913) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The skills, after the first deployment of a console on an organization's own accounts. `deploying-a-console` (1.1) reaches the bucket as the task's own role and keeps no key for the files, names the token's permissions as the dashboard names them today, adds the console's hostname to a zone the organization's site already uses without touching the site, points the connector at the loopback's address and mirrors its image into the organization's repository, waits on the rollout's state rather than the service's, pins the release tag that the organization's own console shares, and says what to do when Git Bash, a token with Read or the connector's warnings get in the way; `migrate` inside the task is offered for a deployment without a pipeline. `building-a-console` (1.2) asks for a square symbol, keeps pictures and fonts as files under the policy, and pins the same release as the deployment. The server behind them takes `S3_CREDENTIALS=platform` for the credentials a platform hands the container (ADR-0018).

## 0.1.8

### Patch Changes

- [`c2dceb8`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/c2dceb83475c6bcc12d7402e74bb97b3f247e80f) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The package carries a third skill, `deploying-a-console`: how an agent puts an organization's console on AWS behind Cloudflare, sized for one person to a hundred, with the monthly cost shown before anything is made, and leaves a repository that redeploys on push. The two other skills now say how the person is involved: the questions they ask, their checkpoints and their verdicts; and the rules every skill shares gained what a person in chat, rather than on the board, is asked and told.

## 0.1.7

### Patch Changes

- [`1a8bc35`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/1a8bc3515db3f131d38a2baf72292010984562a0) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - `console serve [<dir>] [--port <n>]` serves a console of your own from your machine ([ADR-0014](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/blob/main/docs/adr/0014-a-persons-own-console-is-served-from-their-machine-by-the-command.md)): the build in `<dir>` at `http://127.0.0.1:11197/`, and everything under `/api/` carried to the console the command is signed in to with your token, which the pages never see. The loopback only; a request whose host is not it, or that the browser says comes from another site's page, is refused. Without a directory only the API is served, for a development server to send its `/api/` requests to. The serving of a build is now the package's, at `@skillcdn/console/web` (`loadWebRoot`, `WebRoot`, `WebRootError`; Node only), which the image uses for the default UI. The README says how a console of your own is built.

- [`8dca22b`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/8dca22b97549f8c6b4e94796d2b6e07510e0e1a7) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - `GET /api/v1/me` says with what a request acts: `agent` is the name of the token when a token asks, as its person called it, and `null` on a session (`restMeSchema`). The default console, when it holds a token rather than a session (a person's own console, served from their machine), offers nothing a token cannot do: no agents page, no new project, no settings, no changes to members or roles, no sign-out, and the choice of language kept in the browser instead of on the person. The language packs gained `common.notWithToken`.

- [`c4cb88b`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/c4cb88b1f78855a3ee977217f54bc7517393c58d) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The package carries the console's skills in the SkillCDN Format (`skills/working-the-board`, `skills/building-a-console`, with their manifest `SKILLCDN.md`), the specifications they point to (`docs/specs/rest.md`, `docs/specs/cli.md`) and its sources under `src/` without the tests, so that an agent that installed it reads from `node_modules` how the board is worked and how a console is built, at the version installed ([ADR-0015](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/blob/main/docs/adr/0015-the-consoles-skills-live-in-this-repository-in-the-skillcdn-format-and-the-package-carries-them.md)). The same skills are served by SkillCDN at `skillcdn.ai/gh/skillcdn/console`.

## 0.1.6

### Patch Changes

- [`460568a`](https://github.com/skillcdn/console/commit/460568a4c3e97cd02c305f17d89b15954b8b6abd) Thanks [@samo-lucid](https://github.com/samo-lucid)! - The look confirmed, with three things set right: the brand's lockup in the header is smaller (`--sc-brand-size`, 1rem), and the mark on the sign-in page with it; on a phone the tabs wrap instead of running off the edge; and under the wide layout a task's facts come before its text, two to a row, so that its state and its forms are at hand.

## 0.1.5

### Patch Changes
